    extends SceneTree
    var failures := 0
    func _initialize() -> void: call_deferred("run")
    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("RACE PROGRESS FAIL: ", label)
    
    func point_at(track: Track, distance: float) -> Vector2:
        var remaining := fposmod(distance, track.get_total_length())
        for i in track.segment_lengths.size():
            if remaining <= track.segment_lengths[i]:
                return track.segments[i] + track.segment_tangents[i] * remaining
            remaining -= track.segment_lengths[i]
        return track.finish_point
    
    func run() -> void:
        var track: Track = load("res://scenes/Track.tscn").instantiate()
        root.add_child(track)
        var car: Vehicle = load("res://scenes/Vehicle.tscn").instantiate()
        root.add_child(car)
        car.freeze = true
        car.position = track.finish_point
        var tracker := RaceProgress.new()
        var racers: Array[Vehicle] = [car]
        tracker.reset(track, racers)
        var length := track.get_total_length()
        # Reverse full lap and repeatedly oscillate across the start.
        for i in range(1, 201):
            car.position = point_at(track, -length * i / 200.0)
            tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60)
        check(tracker.laps(car) == 0, "reverse lap ignored")
        car.position = track.finish_point
        tracker.reset(track, racers)
        for i in range(20):
            car.position = point_at(track, -5)
            tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60)
            car.position = point_at(track, 5)
            tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60)
        check(tracker.laps(car) == 0, "finish oscillation ignored")
        check(tracker.total_progress(car) < 20, "reverse wrap does not inflate rank")
        car.position = track.finish_point
        tracker.reset(track, racers)
        var finished := false
        for lap in range(3):
            for i in range(1, 201):
                car.position = point_at(track, length * i / 200.0 + 0.1)
                finished = tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60) or finished
            check(tracker.laps(car) == lap + 1, "lap %d increments" % (lap + 1))
            check(finished == (lap == 2), "finish only after final lap")
        car.position = track.finish_point
        tracker.reset(track, racers)
        # Teleport directly to the far end then cross forward: invalid partial lap.
        car.position = point_at(track, length - 5)
        tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60)
        car.position = point_at(track, 5)
        tracker.advance(car, 1.0 / 60)
        check(tracker.laps(car) == 0, "shortcut ignored")
        car.free()
        track.free()
        print("RACE PROGRESS: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
    extends SceneTree
    var failures := 0
    func _initialize() -> void: call_deferred("run")
    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("RACE FINISH FAIL: ", label)
    func run() -> void:
        var original_loadout := LoadoutProfile.read_ids()
        LoadoutProfile.save_ids(&"raider", &"machine_gun")
        change_scene_to_file("res://scenes/Main.tscn")
        await scene_changed
        var game: Game = current_scene
        await create_timer(0.1).timeout
        game.set_physics_process(false)
        for bot in game._bots: bot.set_physics_process(false)
        for car in game.participants: car.freeze = true
        var p := game.vehicle
        var bot: Vehicle = game.participants[1]
        var initial_position := game.get_race_position(p)
        check(initial_position == 6, "initial progress ranking")
        p.position = Vector2(220, 150)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        p.position = Vector2(255, 150)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        p.position = Vector2(285, 150)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        check(game.get_race_position(p) < initial_position, "ranking changes with progress")
        # Complete actual sampled laps through the same physics coordinator API.
        game._reset_race()
        game.set_physics_process(false)
        for driver in game._bots: driver.set_physics_process(false)
        for car in game.participants:
            car.freeze = true
            car._pending_reset = false
        var length := game.track.get_total_length()
        for lap in range(3):
            for i in range(1, 201):
                p.position = point_at(game.track, length * i / 200.0 + 0.1)
                game._physics_process(1.0 / 60)
        check(game.race_over and game.race_status[p] == &"FINISHED", "finish lifecycle")
        check(game.results.visible and game.result.laps == 3, "results shown with 3 laps")
        check(game.result.time > 0 and game.result.position == 1, "result time and rank")
        var final_time := game.elapsed
        game._physics_process(1)
        check(game.elapsed == final_time, "timer stops")
        game.results.get_node("%RetryButton").pressed.emit()
        await process_frame
        check(not game.race_over and not game.results.visible and p.alive and not p.freeze, "Retry fully restores")
        check(game.elapsed == 0 and game.progress.laps(p) == 0, "Retry clears race")
        # Real lethal projectile exercises result creation during Bullet._check_hit.
        for car in game.participants: car.freeze = true
        for index in range(2, game.participants.size()):
            game.participants[index].position = Vector2(600, 400 + index * 20)
        p.position = Vector2(440, 150)
        bot.position = Vector2(300, 150)
        bot.rotation = 0
        bot.gun.config = bot.gun.config.duplicate()
        bot.gun.config.bullet_damage = 100
        bot.gun._shoot()
        await create_timer(0.4).timeout
        check(game.race_over and game.result.status == &"DNF" and game.results.visible, "projectile DNF results")
        game.results.get_node("%GarageButton").pressed.emit()
        await scene_changed
        check(current_scene.scene_file_path == "res://scenes/Garage.tscn", "result Garage action")
        change_scene_to_file("res://scenes/Main.tscn")
        await scene_changed
        game = current_scene
        game.vehicle.take_damage(1000)
        check(game.result.status == &"DNF", "immediate death result")
        game.results.get_node("%MenuButton").pressed.emit()
        await scene_changed
        check(current_scene.scene_file_path == "res://scenes/MainMenu.tscn", "result Menu action")
        current_scene.free()
        LoadoutProfile.save_ids(original_loadout.vehicle, original_loadout.weapon)
        print("RACE FINISH: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
    
    func point_at(track: Track, distance: float) -> Vector2:
        var remaining := fposmod(distance, track.get_total_length())
        for i in track.segment_lengths.size():
            if remaining <= track.segment_lengths[i]:
                return track.segments[i] + track.segment_tangents[i] * remaining
            remaining -= track.segment_lengths[i]
        return track.finish_point

    extends RefCounted
    class_name VehicleDamage
    ## Per-instance runtime state. Shared VehicleConfig is never mutated.
    
    var body: float
    var engine: float
    var wheels: float
    var config: VehicleConfig
    
    func reset(vehicle_config: VehicleConfig) -> void:
        config = vehicle_config
        body = config.max_health
        engine = config.engine_health
        wheels = config.wheels_health
    
    func ratio(module: StringName) -> float:
        match module:
            &"body": return clampf(body / config.max_health, 0.0, 1.0)
            &"engine": return clampf(engine / config.engine_health, 0.0, 1.0)
            &"wheels": return clampf(wheels / config.wheels_health, 0.0, 1.0)
        return 0.0
    
    func apply(amount: float, module: StringName = &"body") -> void:
        if amount <= 0 or not is_finite(amount):
            return
        match module:
            &"body":
                body = maxf(0, body - amount)
                engine = maxf(0, engine - amount * config.engine_damage_share)
                wheels = maxf(0, wheels - amount * config.wheels_damage_share)
            &"engine": engine = maxf(0, engine - amount)
            &"wheels": wheels = maxf(0, wheels - amount)
    
    func repair_wheels(amount: float) -> void:
        if amount > 0.0 and is_finite(amount):
            wheels = minf(config.wheels_health, wheels + amount)
    
    func engine_factor() -> float:
        return lerpf(config.engine_min_factor, 1.0, ratio(&"engine"))
    
    func steering_factor() -> float:
        return lerpf(config.steering_min_factor, 1.0, ratio(&"wheels"))
    
    func traction_factor() -> float:
        return lerpf(config.traction_min_factor, 1.0, ratio(&"wheels"))
    
    func visual_stage() -> int:
        var remaining := ratio(&"body")
        if remaining <= 0: return 4
        if remaining <= 0.25: return 3
        if remaining <= 0.5: return 2
        if remaining <= 0.75: return 1
        return 0
        if not alive:
            return
        ram_boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        boost_time_remaining = maxf(boost_time_remaining, maxf(duration, 0.0))
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func is_boost_active() -> bool:
        return alive and boost_time_remaining > 0.0
    
    
    func activate_ram_boost(duration: float = 9.0) -> void:
        if not alive:
            return
        boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        ram_boost_time_remaining = maxf(ram_boost_time_remaining, maxf(duration, 0.0))
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func is_ram_boost_active() -> bool:
        return alive and ram_boost_time_remaining > 0.0
    
    
    func repair(body_amount: float = 28.0, engine_amount: float = 22.0, wheels_amount: float = 24.0) -> void:
        if not alive or config == null:
            return
        damage.body = minf(config.max_health, damage.body + maxf(body_amount, 0.0))
        damage.engine = minf(config.engine_health, damage.engine + maxf(engine_amount, 0.0))
        damage.wheels = minf(config.wheels_health, damage.wheels + maxf(wheels_amount, 0.0))
        _wheels_repair_cooldown = 0.0
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func has_temporary_weapon() -> bool:
        return alive and weapon_time_remaining > 0.0 and gun != null and gun.enabled
    
    
    func get_current_max_speed() -> float:
        if config == null:
            return 1.0
        return minf(config.max_speed * (config.boost_speed_multiplier if is_boost_active() else 1.0), 200.0)
    
    
    func _update_temporary_bonuses(delta: float) -> void:
        if weapon_time_remaining > 0.0:
            weapon_time_remaining = maxf(0.0, weapon_time_remaining - delta)
    extends Area2D
    class_name BonusPickup
    
    signal collected(pickup: BonusPickup, vehicle: Vehicle)
    
    enum Kind { WEAPON, REPAIR, SPEED_BOOST, RAM_BOOST }
    
    @export var kind: Kind = Kind.WEAPON
    @export var weapon_duration: float = 25.0
    @export var speed_boost_duration: float = 8.0
    @export var ram_boost_duration: float = 9.0
    @export var weapon_config: WeaponConfig
    
    var armed := true
    var collected_once := false
    var _phase := 0.0
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        add_to_group("bonus_pickups")
        collision_layer = 0
        collision_mask = 1
        body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
        monitoring = armed
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func setup(new_kind: Kind, new_weapon: WeaponConfig = null, is_armed: bool = true) -> void:
        kind = new_kind
        weapon_config = new_weapon
        armed = is_armed
        if is_node_ready():
            monitoring = armed
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func set_armed(value: bool) -> void:
        armed = value
        if is_node_ready():
            monitoring = value
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func _process(delta: float) -> void:
        _phase += delta
        rotation = sin(_phase * 1.7) * 0.08
        var pulse_speed := 5.2 if armed else 2.4
        scale = Vector2.ONE * (1.0 + sin(_phase * pulse_speed) * (0.06 if armed else 0.035))
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func collect(vehicle: Vehicle) -> bool:
        if not armed or collected_once or vehicle == null or not vehicle.alive:
            return false
        collected_once = true
        match kind:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                vehicle.activate_temporary_weapon(weapon_config, weapon_duration)
            Kind.REPAIR:
                vehicle.repair()
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                vehicle.activate_boost(speed_boost_duration)
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                vehicle.activate_ram_boost(ram_boost_duration)
        collected.emit(self, vehicle)
        queue_free()
        return true
    
    
    func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
        collect(body as Vehicle)
    
    
    static func kind_label(value: Kind) -> String:
        match value:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                return "ОРУЖИЕ"
            Kind.REPAIR:
                return "РЕМОНТ"
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                return "УСКОРЕНИЕ"
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                return "УСИЛЕННЫЙ ТАРАН"
        return "БОНУС"
    
    
    func _kind_color() -> Color:
        match kind:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                return Color("ef5350")
            Kind.REPAIR:
                return Color("69d47b")
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                return Color("43c8f5")
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                return Color("ff9e2c")
        return Color.WHITE
    
    
    func _draw() -> void:

    func _initialize() -> void:

    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("COLLISION DAMAGE FAIL: ", label)
    
    
    func run() -> void:
        var vehicle := (load("res://scenes/Vehicle.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Vehicle
        root.add_child(vehicle)
        vehicle.add_to_group("combatants")
        await process_frame
    
        var front: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(100.0, 1000.0, 1.0, 1.0, Vector2.RIGHT)
        var side: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(100.0, 1000.0, 1.0, 1.0, Vector2.DOWN)
        var heavy: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(100.0, 1550.0, 1.25, 1.0, Vector2.DOWN)
        var tire: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(100.0, 1000.0, 1.0, 0.3, Vector2.DOWN)
        var harmless: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(20.0, 1000.0, 1.0, 1.0, Vector2.DOWN)
        var catastrophic: Dictionary = vehicle._calculate_collision_damage(400.0, 2000.0, 2.0, 1.2, Vector2.DOWN)
    
        check(float(harmless.damage) == 0.0, "low-speed contact is harmless")
        check(float(front.damage) > 2.0, "medium collision deals visible damage")
        check(float(side.damage) > float(front.damage), "side impact is more dangerous than front")
        check(float(heavy.damage) > float(side.damage), "heavy rammer delivers more damage")
        check(float(tire.damage) < float(side.damage) * 0.4, "tire pile cushions impact")
        check(float(catastrophic.damage) <= vehicle.config.impact_damage_cap, "single-contact damage is capped")
    
        var before := vehicle.health
        var wheels_before := vehicle.damage.wheels
        vehicle._take_collision_damage(float(side.damage), &"wheels", null)
        check(vehicle.health < before, "collision reduces body health")
        check(vehicle.damage.wheels < wheels_before, "side impact damages wheels")
    
        vehicle.free()
        print("COLLISION DAMAGE: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)

    func _initialize() -> void:

    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:

    func run() -> void:

        root.add_child(other)
        check(v.damage.engine_factor() == 1 and v.damage.traction_factor() == 1, "healthy physics unchanged")
        v.take_damage(1000, &"engine")
        check(is_equal_approx(v.damage.engine_factor(), 0.3), "engine minimum")
        check(v.health == v.config.max_health and v.alive, "engine does not kill body")
        v.take_damage(1000, &"wheels")
        check(v.damage.traction_factor() < 1 and v.damage.steering_factor() < 1, "wheels degrade grip and steering")
        check(other.damage.engine_factor() == 1, "shared config does not share damage")
        v.reset_to(Vector2.ZERO, 0)
        for stage in range(1, 5):
            v.take_damage(25)
            check(v.damage.visual_stage() == stage, "visual stage %d" % stage)
        v.apply_control(1, 0, 1, true)
        check(not v.alive and v.throttle == 0 and not v.fire, "dead input blocked")
        await process_frame
        check(not v.gun.is_physics_processing(), "dead weapon disabled")
        v.reset_to(Vector2.ZERO, 0)
        check(v.damage.visual_stage() == 0 and v.damage.engine_factor() == 1 and v.damage.traction_factor() == 1, "reset all modules")
        check(v.gun.is_physics_processing(), "reset weapon")
        v.take_damage(NAN)
        check(v.health == v.config.max_health, "invalid damage rejected")
        v.position = Vector2(0, -1000)
        other.position = Vector2(0, 1000)
        v.take_damage(1000, &"engine")
        v.apply_control(1, 0, 0)
        other.apply_control(1, 0, 0)
        for i in 120:
            await physics_frame
        check(other.get_speed() > v.get_speed() * 1.5, "damaged engine reduces actual acceleration/speed")
        v.apply_control(0, 0, 0)
        other.apply_control(0, 0, 0)
        v.take_damage(1000, &"wheels")
        v.linear_velocity = Vector2(0, 80)
        other.linear_velocity = Vector2(0, 80)
        for i in 10:
            await physics_frame
        check(absf(v.get_lateral_speed()) > absf(other.get_lateral_speed()) + 5, "damaged wheels increase actual drift")
        v.free()
        other.free()
        print("DAMAGE MODULES: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)

    $ sed -n '330,490p' scripts/track.gd
            var stagger := minf(30.0, lane_width * 0.85) * float(index)
            result.append(finish_point + finish_normal * across + finish_tangent * stagger)
        return result
    
    
    func get_bounds() -> Rect2:
        return _bounds
    
    
    func get_total_length() -> float:
        return total_length
    
    
    ## Projects a point onto the centerline.
    ## Returns { segment_index, closest_point, t, distance }.
    func get_closest_segment(point: Vector2) -> Dictionary:
        var min_dist := INF
        var best_idx := 0
        var best_t := 0.0
        var best_point := config.centerline[0]
    
        for i in segment_tangents.size():
            var p1 := segments[i]
            var p2 := config.centerline[(i + 1) % config.centerline.size()]
            var d := p2 - p1
            var seg_len_sq := d.length_squared()
            var t := 0.0
            if seg_len_sq > 0.0:
                t = clampf((point - p1).dot(d) / seg_len_sq, 0.0, 1.0)
            var closest := p1 + d * t
            var dist := point.distance_to(closest)
            if dist < min_dist:
                min_dist = dist
                best_idx = i
                best_t = t
                best_point = closest
    
        return {
            "segment_index": best_idx,
            "closest_point": best_point,
            "t": best_t,
            "distance": min_dist,
        }
    
    
    ## Distance travelled along the centerline (0..total_length) for a point.
    func get_progress_along_track(point: Vector2) -> float:
        var info := get_closest_segment(point)
        var idx: int = info["segment_index"]
        var progress := 0.0
        for i in idx:
            progress += segment_lengths[i]
        progress += segment_lengths[idx] * info["t"]
        return progress
    
    
    ## Samples the racing line by travelled distance. Used by world pickups so
    ## they always land on drivable asphalt.
    func get_point_at_distance(distance: float) -> Vector2:
        if segment_lengths.is_empty() or total_length <= 0.0:
            return get_start_position()
        var remaining := fposmod(distance, total_length)
        for index in segment_lengths.size():
            if remaining <= segment_lengths[index]:
                var next := config.centerline[(index + 1) % config.centerline.size()]
                return segments[index].lerp(next, remaining / segment_lengths[index])
            remaining -= segment_lengths[index]
        return segments[0]
    
    
    func is_point_on_track(point: Vector2, margin: float = 5.0) -> bool:
        return get_closest_segment(point)["distance"] <= (half_width - margin)
    
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Rendering
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    func _draw() -> void:
        if config == null or outer_boundary.size() < 3:
            return
    
        # Track surface. Without a texture it is drawn as a filled ring. With a
        # texture the Line2D ribbon created in _setup_assets() provides the
        # surface, so no polygon fill is needed.
        if config.surface_texture == null:
            draw_colored_polygon(outer_boundary, config.surface_color)
            if inner_boundary.size() >= 3:
                draw_colored_polygon(inner_boundary, config.background_color)
    
        draw_polyline(outer_boundary + PackedVector2Array([outer_boundary[0]]), Color(0.055, 0.042, 0.03, 0.95), 12.0, true)
        draw_polyline(inner_boundary + PackedVector2Array([inner_boundary[0]]), Color(0.055, 0.042, 0.03, 0.95), 12.0, true)
        draw_polyline(outer_boundary + PackedVector2Array([outer_boundary[0]]), config.edge_color, 4.0, true)
        draw_polyline(inner_boundary + PackedVector2Array([inner_boundary[0]]), config.edge_color, 4.0, true)
    
        var lanes := maxi(1, config.lane_count)
        var lane_width := config.track_width / float(lanes)
        for lane in range(1, lanes):
            var offset := -half_width + lane_width * float(lane)
            var divider := _build_offset_loop(offset)
            _draw_dashed_loop(divider, config.centerline_color, 1.0, 14.0, 12.0)
    
        var finish_cells := 12
        var finish_step := config.track_width / float(finish_cells)
        for cell in finish_cells:
            var from := finish_line_end + finish_normal * finish_step * float(cell)
            var color := Color("e5d8bd") if cell % 2 == 0 else Color("292521")
            draw_line(from, from + finish_normal * finish_step, color, 8.0, true)
    
        if show_debug:
            for wp in waypoints:
                draw_arc(wp["position"], wp["radius"], 0.0, TAU, 32, config.waypoint_color, 1.0, true)
                draw_circle(wp["position"], 3.0, config.waypoint_color)
    
    
    func _draw_dashed_loop(points: PackedVector2Array, color: Color, width: float, dash: float, gap: float) -> void:
        var n := points.size()
        for i in n:
            var p1 := points[i]
            var p2 := points[(i + 1) % n]
            var seg := p2 - p1
            var seg_len := seg.length()
            if seg_len <= 0.0:
                continue
            var dir := seg / seg_len
            var dist := 0.0
            var drawing := true
            while dist < seg_len:
                var next_dist: float = minf(dist + (dash if drawing else gap), seg_len)
                if drawing:
                    draw_line(p1 + dir * dist, p1 + dir * next_dist, color, width, true)
                dist = next_dist
                drawing = not drawing
    
    
    func _build_offset_loop(offset: float) -> PackedVector2Array:
        var points := config.centerline
        var result := PackedVector2Array()
        for index in points.size():
            var previous := points[(index - 1 + points.size()) % points.size()]
            var current := points[index]
            var following := points[(index + 1) % points.size()]
            var previous_normal := Vector2(-(current - previous).normalized().y, (current - previous).normalized().x)
            var next_normal := Vector2(-(following - current).normalized().y, (following - current).normalized().x)
            var joined_normal := (previous_normal + next_normal).normalized()
            var alignment := maxf(0.35, joined_normal.dot(next_normal))
            result.append(current + joined_normal * offset / alignment)
        return result
    
    
    func set_debug_enabled(enabled: bool) -> void:
        show_debug = enabled
        queue_redraw()

    +## Replaces polygonal corners with quadratic arcs while preserving long
    +## straights. Dense samples are shared by rendering, physics, AI and progress.
    +func _build_smooth_path(controls: PackedVector2Array) -> PackedVector2Array:
    +	var result := PackedVector2Array()
    +	if controls.size() < 3:
    +		return controls
    +	for index in controls.size():
    +		var previous := controls[(index - 1 + controls.size()) % controls.size()]
    +		var current := controls[index]
    +		var following := controls[(index + 1) % controls.size()]
    +		var incoming := current - previous
    +		var outgoing := following - current
    +		var radius := minf(82.0, minf(incoming.length() * 0.28, outgoing.length() * 0.28))
    +		var entry := current - incoming.normalized() * radius
    +		var exit := current + outgoing.normalized() * radius
    +		result.append(entry)
    +		for step in range(1, 7):
    +			var t := float(step) / 6.0
    +			var one_minus := 1.0 - t
    +			result.append(entry * one_minus * one_minus + current * 2.0 * one_minus * t + exit * t * t)
    +	return result
    +
    +
     ## Builds an offset boundary of the centerline on one side.

## Assistant

Следующий блок — интерфейс и баланс. Нижнюю центральную панель убираю полностью: спидометр, бонус и состояние машины объединяются в один компактный приборный модуль в правом нижнем углу. Мини-карта остаётся сверху справа, гоночная информация — сверху по центру. Параллельно бонусы ускорены вдвое, круг полностью ремонтирует машину, столкновения стали существенно опаснее, а пять ботов получили пять разных ролей.

Первые проверки выявили три побочных эффекта сглаживания: старый алгоритм границ добавлял лишние дуги поверх уже сглаженной линии, один агрессивный бот слишком рано бросал гонку ради тарана, а безопасный порог столкновения стал чрезмерно низким. Корректирую: границы теперь строятся непосредственно по нормалям плавной траектории, таранщик атакует только удобную цель впереди, а разрушительный урон начинается с ощутимой скорости, а не с парковочного контакта.

        import re,math
        s=open('resources/iron_circuit.tres').read(); nums=list(map(float,re.search(r'centerline = PackedVector2Array\(([^)]*)\)',s).group(1).split(',')))
        c=list(zip(nums[::2],nums[1::2])); pts=[]
        for i,p in enumerate(c):
         prev=c[i-1]; fol=c[(i+1)%len(c)]
         inc=(p[0]-prev[0],p[1]-prev[1]); out=(fol[0]-p[0],fol[1]-p[1])
         il=math.hypot(*inc); ol=math.hypot(*out); rad=min(82,il*.28,ol*.28)
         en=(p[0]-inc[0]/il*rad,p[1]-inc[1]/il*rad); ex=(p[0]+out[0]/ol*rad,p[1]+out[1]/ol*rad)
         pts.append(en)
         for step in range(1,7):
          t=step/6; q=1-t; pts.append((en[0]*q*q+p[0]*2*q*t+ex[0]*t*t,en[1]*q*q+p[1]*2*q*t+ex[1]*t*t))
        def norm(v):
         l=math.hypot(*v);return(v[0]/l,v[1]/l)
        def distseg(p,a,b):
         dx=b[0]-a[0];dy=b[1]-a[1];ll=dx*dx+dy*dy;t=max(0,min(1,((p[0]-a[0])*dx+(p[1]-a[1])*dy)/ll));q=(a[0]+dx*t,a[1]+dy*t);return math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1])
        for side in (1,-1):
         for i,p in enumerate(pts):
          pr=pts[i-1];nx=pts[(i+1)%len(pts)];t1=norm((p[0]-pr[0],p[1]-pr[1]));t2=norm((nx[0]-p[0],nx[1]-p[1]));n1=(-t1[1],t1[0]);n2=(-t2[1],t2[0]);n=norm((n1[0]+n2[0],n1[1]+n2[1]));b=(p[0]+n[0]*105*side,p[1]+n[1]*105*side)
          d=min(distseg(b,pts[j],pts[(j+1)%len(pts)]) for j in range(len(pts)))
          if abs(d-105)>50: print(side,i,p,b,d)
        PY

    extends SceneTree
    func _initialize(): call_deferred("run")
    func run():
    	var game: Game = load("res://scenes/Main.tscn").instantiate()
    	root.add_child(game)
    	await process_frame
    	game.set_physics_process(false)
    	for bot in game._bots: bot.set_physics_process(false)
    	for car in game.participants: car.freeze = true; car._pending_reset = false
    	print("finish ", game.track.finish_point, " length ", game.track.total_length)
    	for i in game.participants.size():
    		var c=game.participants[i]; print("start ",i," pos ",c._reset_position," prog ",game.progress.entries[c])
    	var p=game.vehicle
    	print("rank0 ",game.get_race_position(p))
    	for q in [Vector2(220,150),Vector2(255,150),Vector2(285,150)]:
    		p.position=q; game.progress.advance(p,1.0/60); print("move ",q," entry ",game.progress.entries[p]," rank ",game.get_race_position(p))
    	game._reset_race(); game.set_physics_process(false)
    	for d in game._bots: d.set_physics_process(false)
    	for c in game.participants: c.freeze=true; c._pending_reset=false
    	var length=game.track.total_length
    	for lap in 3:
    		for i in range(1,201):
    			p.position=point_at(game.track,length*i/200.0+0.1); game._physics_process(1.0/60)
    		print("lap sim ",lap," laps=",game.progress.laps(p)," entry=",game.progress.entries[p])
    	game.free(); quit()
    func point_at(track: Track, distance: float):
    	var remaining=fposmod(distance,track.total_length)
    	for i in track.segment_lengths.size():
    		if remaining<=track.segment_lengths[i]: return track.segments[i]+track.segment_tangents[i]*remaining
    		remaining-=track.segment_lengths[i]
    	return track.finish_point

    extends SceneTree
    func _initialize() -> void: call_deferred("run")
    func run() -> void:
        for resolution in [Vector2i(800, 600), Vector2i(1024, 768), Vector2i(1366, 768)]:
            for mode in ["Main", "ArenaBattle"]:
                var view := SubViewport.new()
                view.size = resolution
                view.render_target_update_mode = SubViewport.UPDATE_ALWAYS
                root.add_child(view)
                var game: Node = load("res://scenes/%s.tscn" % mode).instantiate()
                view.add_child(game)
                if mode == "Main": game._debug_enabled = true
                await create_timer(0.25).timeout
                await RenderingServer.frame_post_draw
                view.get_texture().get_image().save_png("res://docs/sprints/bcd/%s-%d.png" % [mode, resolution.x])
                view.free()
        quit()
        strip._on_body_entered(vehicle)
        check(vehicle.damage.wheels < before_spikes, "spike strip damages wheels")
        vehicle.free()
    
        var race := (load("res://scenes/Main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Game
        root.add_child(race)
        await process_frame
        check(race.participants.size() == 6, "race has player and five bots")
        var strategies := {}
        for bot in get_nodes_in_group("bots"):
            strategies[(bot as BotController).strategy] = true
        check(strategies.size() >= 3, "race bots use distinct strategies")
        race.free()
    
        var survival := (load("res://scenes/ArenaBattle.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Survival
        root.add_child(survival)
        await process_frame
        check(survival._enemies.size() == 5, "survival has five bots")
        var arena_strategies := {}
        for bot in get_nodes_in_group("arena_bots"):
            arena_strategies[(bot as ArenaBot).strategy] = true
        check(arena_strategies.size() >= 4, "arena bots use distinct strategies")
        print("VEHICLE PROGRESSION: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
    extends SceneTree
    var failures := 0
    func _initialize() -> void: call_deferred("run")
    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("HUD FAIL: ", label)
    func run() -> void:
        for resolution in [Vector2i(800, 600), Vector2i(1024, 768), Vector2i(1366, 768)]:
            for scene in ["Main", "ArenaBattle"]:
                var view := SubViewport.new()
                view.size = resolution
                root.add_child(view)
                var game: Node = load("res://scenes/%s.tscn" % scene).instantiate()
                if scene == "Main": game.spawn_bots = false
                view.add_child(game)
                await physics_frame
                await process_frame
                await process_frame
                var v: Vehicle = game.vehicle if scene == "Main" else game.player
                game.set_physics_process(false)
                var hud: CanvasLayer = game.hud
                if scene == "Main": hud.update_info(v, {}, true)
                else: hud.update_status(v, 2, 1)
                check(hud.readout.player == v, "actual player bound")
                v.linear_velocity = Vector2(128, 0)
                v.take_damage(20)
                v.take_damage(10, &"engine")
                if scene == "Main": hud.update_info(v, {}, true)
                else: hud.update_status(v, 2, 1)
                check("128" in hud.readout.speed.text, "runtime speed")
                check(hud.modules.bars[0].value < 100 and hud.modules.bars[0].value > 0, "runtime body")
                check(hud.modules.bars[1].value < 100 and hud.modules.bars[2].value < 100, "runtime modules")
                var player_rect := Rect2(v.get_global_transform_with_canvas().origin - Vector2(22, 14), Vector2(44, 28))
                var rects: Array[Rect2] = hud.occupied_rects()
                for rect in rects:
                    check(not rect.intersects(player_rect), "%s %s spawn unobstructed" % [scene, resolution])
                    check(not rect.has_point(Vector2(resolution) / 2), "center unobstructed")
                    check(Rect2(Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(resolution)).encloses(rect), "within viewport")
                if scene == "Main":
                    var debug_rect: Rect2 = hud.get_node("DebugPanel").get_global_rect()
                    for rect in rects: check(not debug_rect.intersects(rect), "F1 separate from HUD")
                view.free()
        print("HUD RACE: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)

## Assistant

Все указанные правки внесены.

- Фон затемнён и отделён по тону от трассы и машин.
- Острые вершины заменены настоящими плавными радиусами. Одна сглаженная траектория используется графикой, физикой, ботами и подсчётом прогресса.
- Исправлен пересчёт позиции после обгонов.
- Исправлено прохождение и отображение кругов.
- После каждого завершённого круга кузов, двигатель и колёса восстанавливаются до 100%.
- HUD собран в единый компактный блок справа внизу. Центральная нижняя панель удалена.
- Бонусы появляются каждые четыре секунды вместо восьми.
- Столкновения стали значительно разрушительнее, но небольшие парковочные контакты остаются безопасными.
- Пять ботов получили уникальные тактики:
  - чистый гонщик;
  - агрессивный таранщик;
  - охотник за бонусами;
  - блокирующий соперников;
  - скоростной спринтер.
- Мини-карта и верхняя гоночная панель сохранены.

Добавлены проверки обгона трёх участников, полного ремонта после круга и пяти уникальных тактик ботов. Все профильные тесты проходят: геометрия, круги, финиш, позиция, HUD, боты, бонусы, столкновения и боевой режим.

Игра оставлена запущенной в режиме «Гонка» для просмотра.

## User

Фон по прежнему сливается с моделькой машины. Есть артефакты границ траектории трассы на изгибе. Скорость появления бонусов нужно сделать более быстрой. Время жизни бонусов так же нужно увеличить.

## Assistant

Исправляю точечно три причины: фон сделаю холоднее, темнее и менее конкурентным по детализации; визуальные границы трассы переведу на отдельные линии с круглыми соединениями, чтобы убрать шипы и разрывы на дугах; систему бонусов переделаю на несколько одновременно активных объектов, иначе увеличение времени жизни фактически замедляет появление новых. Целевые параметры: новый бонус примерно каждые 2,5 секунды, до трёх на трассе, время жизни — 22 секунды.

    +func _register_active(pickup: BonusPickup) -> void:
    +	if not _active_pickups.has(pickup):
    +		_active_pickups.append(pickup)
    +	_active_lifetimes[pickup] = active_lifetime
    +
    +
    +func _update_active_pickups(delta: float) -> void:
    +	for pickup in _active_pickups.duplicate():
    +		if not is_instance_valid(pickup):
    +			_active_pickups.erase(pickup)
    +			_active_lifetimes.erase(pickup)
    +			continue
    +		var remaining := float(_active_lifetimes.get(pickup, active_lifetime)) - delta
    +		if remaining <= 0.0:
    +			_active_pickups.erase(pickup)
    +			_active_lifetimes.erase(pickup)
    +			pickup.queue_free()
    +		else:
    +			_active_lifetimes[pickup] = remaining

    func _setup_surface_line() -> void:
        if config.surface_texture == null:
            return
        _surface_line = Line2D.new()
        _surface_line.name = "SurfaceTexture"
        _surface_line.points = path_points
        _surface_line.closed = true
        _surface_line.width = config.track_width
        _surface_line.joint_mode = Line2D.LINE_JOINT_ROUND
        _surface_line.begin_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
        _surface_line.end_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
        _surface_line.texture_mode = Line2D.LINE_TEXTURE_TILE
        _surface_line.texture = config.surface_texture
        _surface_line.modulate = Color(0.9, 0.92, 0.94, 1.0)
        _surface_line.texture_repeat = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_REPEAT_ENABLED
        _surface_line.texture_filter = CanvasItem.TEXTURE_FILTER_LINEAR_WITH_MIPMAPS
        _surface_line.z_index = -5
        add_child(_surface_line)
    
    
    func _setup_edge_lines() -> void:
        _add_edge_line(inner_boundary, 14.0, Color(0.035, 0.03, 0.025, 0.98), -4)
        _add_edge_line(outer_boundary, 14.0, Color(0.035, 0.03, 0.025, 0.98), -4)
        _add_edge_line(inner_boundary, 4.0, config.edge_color, -3)
        _add_edge_line(outer_boundary, 4.0, config.edge_color, -3)
    
    
    func _add_edge_line(points: PackedVector2Array, width: float, color: Color, layer: int) -> void:
        var line := Line2D.new()
        line.points = points
        line.closed = true
        line.width = width
        line.default_color = color
        line.joint_mode = Line2D.LINE_JOINT_ROUND
        line.begin_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
        line.end_cap_mode = Line2D.LINE_CAP_ROUND
        line.antialiased = true
        line.z_index = layer
        add_child(line)

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Rendering (local space; the node transform handles rotation)

    func _draw() -> void:
        if config == null:
            return
    
        _draw_boost_flame()
        # Keep collision and physics dimensions unchanged while giving the new
        # vehicle silhouettes enough screen presence to read during a race.
        var visual_length := config.length * VISUAL_SCALE
        var visual_width := config.width * VISUAL_SCALE
        var half_l := visual_length * 0.5
        var body_rect := Rect2(
            Vector2(-half_l, -visual_width * 0.5),
            Vector2(visual_length, visual_width)
        )
    
        if config.texture != null:
            # Image-driven look: swap the texture in VehicleConfig, no code changes.
            draw_texture_rect(config.texture, body_rect, false)
        else:
            var vertices := PackedVector2Array([
                Vector2(config.length * 0.5 - config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio, config.width * 0.5),
                Vector2(config.length * 0.5, config.width * 0.5 - config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio),
                Vector2(config.length * 0.5, -config.width * 0.5 + config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio),
                Vector2(config.length * 0.5 - config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio, -config.width * 0.5),
                Vector2(-config.length * 0.5 + config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio, -config.width * 0.5),
                Vector2(-config.length * 0.5, -config.width * 0.5 + config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio),
                Vector2(-config.length * 0.5, config.width * 0.5 - config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio),
                Vector2(-config.length * 0.5 + config.width * config.corner_bevel_ratio, config.width * 0.5),
            ])
            for i in vertices.size():
                vertices[i] *= VISUAL_SCALE
            draw_colored_polygon(vertices, config.body_color)
            var closed := vertices + PackedVector2Array([vertices[0]])
            draw_polyline(closed, config.outline_color, 2.0, true)
        _draw_ram_boost(visual_length, visual_width)
        _draw_active_weapon(visual_length)
        if not config.debug_vectors:
            return
        draw_circle(Vector2(half_l, 0.0), 3.0, config.front_marker_color)
        draw_line(Vector2.ZERO, Vector2(half_l * 1.5, 0.0), Color.WHITE, 2.0, true)
    
        var local_velocity := linear_velocity.rotated(-rotation) * _velocity_debug_scale
        draw_line(Vector2.ZERO, local_velocity, config.velocity_vector_color, 1.0, true)

    +func _body_vertices(body_length: float, body_width: float) -> PackedVector2Array:

    +	float detail = (luminance - 0.34) * 0.32;
    +	vec3 graphite = vec3(0.40, 0.46, 0.52) + vec3(detail);
    +	graphite = clamp(graphite, vec3(0.31), vec3(0.60));
    +	COLOR = vec4(graphite, tex.a);
    +}
    +"""
    +	var asphalt_material := ShaderMaterial.new()
    +	asphalt_material.shader = asphalt_shader

## User

Фиолетовая машинка часто оказывается на старте вне трассы. Нужно переработать логику ботов - их основая задача завершить круги первыми. Если необходимо то таранить другие машинки или их обстрелять если есть бонус оружение.

## Assistant

Исправлю две связанные причины: расстановку на старте сделаю строго по ширине трассы с безопасным запасом от границ, а приоритет ботов перестрою на прохождение круга. Таран и стрельба останутся вторичными действиями — только когда соперник находится впереди и атака не уводит бота с гоночной траектории. Сначала проверяю текущую логику стартовых слотов и принятия решений.

    scripts/race_progress.gd:24:    var on_track: bool = track.get_closest_segment(point).distance <= track.half_width + track.config.progress_edge_margin
    scripts/race_progress.gd:25:    var was_on_track: bool = track.get_closest_segment(previous).distance <= track.half_width + track.config.progress_edge_margin

    scripts/race_progress.gd:39:            e.checkpoint += 1

    scripts/race_progress.gd:41:    var after_side := (point - track.finish_point).dot(track.finish_tangent)
    scripts/race_progress.gd:42:    var forward_cross := before_side < 0 and after_side >= 0 and motion.dot(track.finish_tangent) > 0
    scripts/race_progress.gd:43:    if forward_cross and e.checkpoint == 4 and e.travelled >= length * track.config.minimum_lap_fraction:

    $ sed -n '1,260p' scripts/bot_controller.gd && sed -n '1,260p' scripts/game.gd && sed -n '1,220p' scripts/track.gd && sed -n '1,150p' tests/bot_smoke.gd
    extends Node
    class_name BotController
    ## Waypoint-following AI driver for a [Vehicle].
    ##
    ## Produces the same command contract as the player (`throttle, brake, steer,
    ## fire`) and feeds it straight into [method Vehicle.apply_control], so bots
    ## reuse the exact vehicle physics and weapon logic with no special cases.
    
    ## Track used as the racing line. Injected by [Game].
    @export var track: Track
    ## Vehicle the bot aims at (usually the player). Injected by [Game].
    @export var target: Vehicle
    
    enum Strategy { RACER, BRAWLER, OPPORTUNIST, BLOCKER, SPRINTER }
    @export var strategy: Strategy = Strategy.RACER
    
    @export_group("Driving")
    ## Throttle held while the bot is aligned with the racing line.
    @export_range(0.0, 1.0, 0.05) var cruise_throttle: float = 0.9
    ## Distance ahead along the centreline used as the steering target.
    @export_range(10.0, 400.0, 5.0) var lookahead: float = 110.0
    ## Heading error (radians) above which the bot eases off the throttle.
    @export_range(0.0, 3.0, 0.05) var ease_angle: float = 0.5
    ## Heading error (radians) above which the bot brakes into the corner.
    @export_range(0.0, 3.0, 0.05) var brake_angle: float = 1.2
    ## Steering gain: steer = clamp(heading_error * gain, -1, 1).
    @export_range(0.1, 10.0, 0.1) var steer_gain: float = 2.5
    
    @export_group("Recovery")
    ## Speed below which, while trying to drive, the bot is considered stuck.
    @export_range(0.0, 200.0, 5.0) var stuck_speed: float = 20.0
    ## Seconds of being stuck before the bot backs up.
    @export_range(0.0, 10.0, 0.1) var stuck_timeout: float = 1.5
    ## Seconds spent reversing during a recovery.
    @export_range(0.0, 10.0, 0.1) var reverse_duration: float = 0.9
    
    @export_group("Combat")
    ## Maximum distance at which the bot opens fire.
    @export_range(0.0, 3000.0, 10.0) var fire_range: float = 450.0
    ## Maximum angle (radians) between the nose and the target to open fire.
    @export_range(0.0, 3.14, 0.01) var fire_angle: float = 0.2
    
    var vehicle: Vehicle
    
    var _start_position: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var _start_angle: float = 0.0
    var _stuck_timer: float = 0.0
    var _reversing: float = 0.0
    var _lane_bias := 0.0
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        vehicle = get_parent() as Vehicle
        _apply_strategy()
        if vehicle != null:
            _start_position = vehicle.global_position
            _start_angle = vehicle.rotation
    
    
    func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
        if vehicle == null or track == null:
            return
        var commands := get_commands(delta)
        vehicle.apply_control(
            commands["throttle"], commands["brake"], commands["steer"], commands["fire"]
        )
    
    
    ## Wires the bot to its world. Called by [Game] once the scene is ready.
    func setup(track_ref: Track, target_ref: Vehicle) -> void:
        track = track_ref
        target = target_ref
    
    
    ## Returns the current command dict. `delta` only advances the recovery timer,
    ## so tests can inspect the decision without side effects by passing 0.
    func get_commands(delta: float = 0.0) -> Dictionary:
        var throttle := 0.0
        var brake := 0.0
        var steer := clampf(_heading_error() * steer_gain, -1.0, 1.0)
    
        if _reversing > 0.0:
            _reversing = maxf(0.0, _reversing - delta)
            throttle = -0.7
            steer = -steer
        else:
            var magnitude := absf(_heading_error())
            if magnitude >= brake_angle:
                brake = 0.6
            elif magnitude >= ease_angle:
                throttle = cruise_throttle * 0.4
            else:
                throttle = cruise_throttle
    
            if vehicle.get_speed() < stuck_speed and throttle > 0.0:
                _stuck_timer += delta
            else:
                _stuck_timer = 0.0
            if _stuck_timer >= stuck_timeout:
                _stuck_timer = 0.0
                _reversing = reverse_duration
    
        return {
            "throttle": throttle,
            "brake": brake,
            "steer": steer,
            "fire": _should_fire(),
        }
    
    
    ## Returns the bot to the position it had when the scene loaded.
    func reset() -> void:
        _stuck_timer = 0.0
        _reversing = 0.0
        if vehicle != null:
            vehicle.reset_to(_start_position, _start_angle)
    
    
    func _heading_error() -> float:
        if vehicle == null or track == null:
            return 0.0
        var target_point := _lookahead_point(vehicle.global_position, lookahead)
        var line_info := track.get_closest_segment(target_point)
        target_point += track.segment_normals[int(line_info.segment_index)] * _lane_bias * track.half_width
        if strategy == Strategy.OPPORTUNIST:
            var pickup := _nearest_pickup(150.0)
            if pickup != null:
                target_point = pickup.global_position
        elif strategy == Strategy.BRAWLER:
            var victim := _combat_target()
            if victim != null:
                var to_victim := victim.global_position - vehicle.global_position
                var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(vehicle.rotation)
                if to_victim.length() < 85.0 and forward.dot(to_victim.normalized()) > 0.55:
                    target_point = victim.global_position
        elif strategy == Strategy.BLOCKER and is_instance_valid(target) and target.alive:
            var target_distance := vehicle.global_position.distance_to(target.global_position)
            if target_distance < 220.0:
                target_point = target.global_position + Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(target.rotation) * 45.0
        var to_target := target_point - vehicle.global_position
        if to_target.length_squared() < 0.0001:
            return 0.0
        var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(vehicle.rotation)
        return forward.angle_to(to_target)
    
    
    ## Walks the centreline forward from the closest point and returns a point
    ## `distance` px ahead, giving a smooth racing line even on sparse tracks.
    func _lookahead_point(from: Vector2, distance: float) -> Vector2:
        var info := track.get_closest_segment(from)
        var idx: int = info["segment_index"]
        var count := track.segment_lengths.size()
        if count == 0:
            return from
    
        var t: float = float(info["t"])
        var remaining := distance
        var current_remaining := track.segment_lengths[idx] * (1.0 - t)
        if remaining <= current_remaining:
            var p1: Vector2 = track.segments[idx]
            var p2: Vector2 = track.path_points[(idx + 1) % count]
            var local_t := t + remaining / track.segment_lengths[idx]
            return p1.lerp(p2, clampf(local_t, 0.0, 1.0))
    
        remaining -= current_remaining
        var i := (idx + 1) % count
        var guard := 0
        while remaining > track.segment_lengths[i] and guard < count:
            remaining -= track.segment_lengths[i]
            i = (i + 1) % count
            guard += 1
        var p1_next: Vector2 = track.segments[i]
        var p2_next: Vector2 = track.path_points[(i + 1) % count]
        return p1_next.lerp(p2_next, clampf(remaining / track.segment_lengths[i], 0.0, 1.0))
    
    
    func _should_fire() -> bool:
        if strategy == Strategy.SPRINTER:
            return false
        var victim := _combat_target()
        if vehicle == null or victim == null or not is_instance_valid(victim):
            return false
        var to_target := victim.global_position - vehicle.global_position
        var distance := to_target.length()
        if distance > fire_range or distance < 0.001:
            return false
        var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(vehicle.rotation)
        return absf(forward.angle_to(to_target)) <= fire_angle
    
    func _apply_strategy() -> void:
        match strategy:
            Strategy.RACER:
                cruise_throttle = 0.96
                lookahead = 135.0
                ease_angle = 0.58
                _lane_bias = -0.48
            Strategy.BRAWLER:
                cruise_throttle = 1.0
                lookahead = 80.0
                fire_angle = 0.34
                fire_range = 360.0
                _lane_bias = -0.18
            Strategy.OPPORTUNIST:
                lookahead = 95.0
                fire_range = 560.0
                fire_angle = 0.26
                _lane_bias = 0.18
            Strategy.BLOCKER:
                cruise_throttle = 0.92
                lookahead = 105.0
                fire_range = 420.0
                fire_angle = 0.3
                _lane_bias = 0.48
            Strategy.SPRINTER:
                cruise_throttle = 1.0
                lookahead = 165.0
                ease_angle = 0.72
                brake_angle = 1.48
                steer_gain = 2.2
                _lane_bias = 0.0
    
    func _combat_target() -> Vehicle:
        if strategy == Strategy.RACER and is_instance_valid(target) and target.alive:
            return target
        var best: Vehicle = null
        var score := INF
        for node in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("combatants"):
            var candidate := node as Vehicle
            if candidate == null or candidate == vehicle or not candidate.alive:
                continue
            var distance := vehicle.global_position.distance_to(candidate.global_position)
            var candidate_score := distance
            if strategy == Strategy.OPPORTUNIST:
                candidate_score *= 0.35 + 0.65 * candidate.damage.ratio(&"body")
            if candidate_score < score:
                score = candidate_score
                best = candidate
        return best
    
    func _nearest_pickup(radius: float) -> BonusPickup:
        var best: BonusPickup = null
        var best_distance := radius * radius
        for node in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("bonus_pickups"):
            var pickup := node as BonusPickup
            if pickup == null:
                continue
            var distance := vehicle.global_position.distance_squared_to(pickup.global_position)
            if distance < best_distance:
                best_distance = distance
                best = pickup
        return best
    extends Node2D
    class_name Game
    ## Main coordinator for the Godot port.
    ##
    ## Mirrors the Phase 4 Python `Game`: it wires up the track, vehicle, camera
    ## and HUD, feeds player commands into the vehicle and handles restart.
    ## Physics runs on Godot's fixed 60 Hz tick (see Project Settings -> Physics).
    
    @onready var track: Track = $Track
    @onready var vehicle: Vehicle = $Vehicle
    @onready var camera: FollowCamera = $Camera
    @onready var audio: AudioManager = $AudioManager
    @onready var hud: DebugHUD = $HUD
    @onready var bullets: Node2D = $Bullets
    
    ## Toggle the AI opponents. Tests disable them to keep physics deterministic.
    @export var spawn_bots: bool = true
    
    var _input_handler := InputHandler.new()
    var _debug_enabled := false
    var _bots: Array = []
    var participants: Array[Vehicle] = []
    var race_status: Dictionary = {}
    var kills: Dictionary = {}
    var damage_dealt: Dictionary = {}
    var progress := RaceProgress.new()
    var elapsed := 0.0
    var race_over := false
    var finish_order: Array[Vehicle] = []
    var result: Dictionary = {}
    @onready var results: Control = $Results/RaceResults
    var bonus_spawner: BonusSpawner
    var _countdown_remaining := 0.0
    var _countdown_step := 0
    var _last_lap_announced := false
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        if DisplayServer.get_name() != "headless":
            _apply_loadout()
        bonus_spawner = BonusSpawner.new()
        bonus_spawner.name = "BonusSpawner"
        bonus_spawner.spawn_interval = 2.5
        bonus_spawner.first_spawn_delay = 2.0
        bonus_spawner.warning_duration = 0.7
        bonus_spawner.active_lifetime = 22.0
        bonus_spawner.max_active = 3
        add_child(bonus_spawner)
        bonus_spawner.setup_for_track(track)
        results.retry_requested.connect(_reset_race)
        vehicle.impacted.connect(audio.play_collision)
        vehicle.impacted.connect(_on_vehicle_impact)
        if vehicle.gun != null:
            vehicle.gun.fired.connect(_on_weapon_fired)
        if spawn_bots:
            _bots = get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("bots")
            for bot in _bots:
                (bot as BotController).setup(track, vehicle)
                (bot.get_parent() as Vehicle).impacted.connect(audio.play_collision)
        else:
            for bot in get_tree().get_nodes_in_group("bots"):
                bot.get_parent().queue_free()
        participants.append(vehicle)
        for bot in _bots:
            participants.append(bot.get_parent() as Vehicle)
        track.config.lane_count = participants.size()
        track.queue_redraw()
        for car in participants:
            car.died.connect(_on_racer_died)
            car.damaged.connect(_on_racer_damaged)
        hud.configure_race(track, participants)
        _reset_race()
    
    
    func _apply_loadout() -> void:
        var ids := LoadoutProfile.read_ids()
        var vehicle_id: StringName = ids.vehicle if PlayerProfile.is_owned(&"vehicle", ids.vehicle) else &"raider"
        vehicle.config = PlayerProfile.apply_vehicle_upgrades(LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(vehicle_id))
    
    func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
        var commands := _input_handler.get_commands()
        if commands["restart"]:
            _reset_race()
            return
        if race_over: return
        if _countdown_remaining > 0.0:
            _update_countdown(delta)
            vehicle.apply_control(0.0, 1.0, 0.0, false)
            return
        elapsed += delta
        for car in participants:
            if race_status[car] == &"RACING":
                var previous_laps := progress.laps(car)
                var finished := progress.advance(car, delta)
                if progress.laps(car) > previous_laps:
                    car.repair_full()
                    if car == vehicle and not finished:
                        hud.show_banner("КРУГ ПРОЙДЕН · ПОЛНЫЙ РЕМОНТ", 1.4)
                if finished:
                    _finish_racer(car)
                    if race_over: return
        if not _last_lap_announced and progress.laps(vehicle) == track.config.laps - 1:
            _last_lap_announced = true
            hud.show_banner("ПОСЛЕДНИЙ КРУГ", 1.6)
        var throttle: float = commands["throttle"]
        var brake: float = commands["brake"]
        # Arcade reverse: holding the brake key at a standstill backs the car up.
        # Without this the player can get permanently wedged into a corner.
        if brake > 0.0 and vehicle.get_forward_speed() < 20.0:
            throttle = -0.6
            brake = 0.0
        vehicle.apply_control(throttle, brake, commands["steer"], commands["fire"])
    
    
    func _unhandled_input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
        if event.is_action_pressed("ui_cancel"):
            get_tree().change_scene_to_file("res://scenes/MainMenu.tscn")
    
    
    func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
        if _input_handler.is_debug_toggle_pressed():
            _debug_enabled = not _debug_enabled
            track.set_debug_enabled(_debug_enabled)
        hud.update_info(vehicle, _input_handler.get_commands(), _debug_enabled)
        hud.update_race(get_race_position(vehicle), participants.size(), mini(progress.laps(vehicle) + 1, track.config.laps), track.config.laps, elapsed)
        audio.set_engine_intensity(_engine_intensity())
    
    
    ## Rebuilds the race state: puts the vehicle back on the start line.
    func _reset_race() -> void:
        race_over = false
        elapsed = 0.0
        finish_order.clear()
        result.clear()
        _last_lap_announced = false
        results.hide()
        if bonus_spawner != null:
            bonus_spawner.reset()
        var start_angle := track.get_start_angle()
        var start_grid := track.get_start_grid(participants.size())
        _clear_bullets()
        for bot in _bots:
            (bot as BotController).reset()
            bot.set_physics_process(true)
        for index in participants.size():
            participants[index].reset_to(start_grid[index], start_angle)
        for car in participants:
            race_status[car] = &"RACING"
            kills[car] = 0
            damage_dealt[car] = 0.0
            if track.config.combat_enabled:
                car.add_to_group("combatants")
            else:
                car.remove_from_group("combatants")
        # Use intended reset positions before the deferred physics teleport executes.
        progress.reset(track, participants)
        for car in participants:
            var entry: Dictionary = progress.entries[car]
            entry.position = car._reset_position
            entry.projection = track.get_progress_along_track(car._reset_position)
            entry.progress = entry.projection
        camera.target = vehicle
        camera.set_bounds(track.get_bounds())
        camera.snap_to_target()
        if DisplayServer.get_name() != "headless":
            _countdown_remaining = 3.2
            _countdown_step = 3
            hud.show_banner("3", 0.7)
            for bot in _bots:
                bot.set_physics_process(false)
        else:
            _countdown_remaining = 0.0
    
    
    func _update_countdown(delta: float) -> void:
        var before := ceili(_countdown_remaining)
        _countdown_remaining = maxf(0.0, _countdown_remaining - delta)
        var after := ceili(_countdown_remaining)
        if after != before and after > 0 and after <= 3:
            _countdown_step = after
            hud.show_banner(str(after), 0.7)
        if _countdown_remaining <= 0.0:
            hud.show_banner("СТАРТ!", 0.9)
            for bot in _bots:
                bot.set_physics_process(true)
    
    
    func _clear_bullets(deferred: bool = false) -> void:
        for bullet in bullets.get_children():
            if deferred: bullet.queue_free()
            else: bullet.free()
    
    
    func _on_weapon_fired() -> void:
        audio.play_fire()
    
    func _on_vehicle_impact(intensity: float) -> void:
        camera.shake(3.0 + intensity * 7.0, 0.12)
    
    
    func _engine_intensity() -> float:
        if vehicle.config == null or vehicle.config.max_speed <= 0.0:
            return 0.0
        return clampf(absf(vehicle.get_forward_speed()) / vehicle.config.max_speed, 0.0, 1.0)
    
    
    func _on_racer_damaged(_target: Vehicle, amount: float, source: Node) -> void:
        if is_instance_valid(source) and damage_dealt.has(source):
            damage_dealt[source] += amount
    
    
    func _on_racer_died(car: Vehicle) -> void:
        race_status[car] = &"DNF"
        if is_instance_valid(car.last_attacker) and kills.has(car.last_attacker):
            kills[car.last_attacker] += 1
        audio.play_explosion()
        if car == vehicle: _end_race()
    
    
    func _finish_racer(car: Vehicle) -> void:
        race_status[car] = &"FINISHED"
        finish_order.append(car)
        _stop_racer(car)
        if car == vehicle:
            audio.play_finish()
            _end_race()
    
    
    func _stop_racer(car: Vehicle) -> void:
        car.apply_control(0, 0, 0, false)
        car.remove_from_group("combatants")
        if car.gun != null: car.gun.set_firing(false)
        car.linear_velocity = Vector2.ZERO
        car.angular_velocity = 0
        call_deferred("_freeze_racer", car)
        var driver := car.get_node_or_null("BotController")
        if driver != null: driver.set_physics_process(false)
    
    
    func get_race_position(car: Vehicle) -> int:
        var ordered := participants.duplicate()
        ordered.sort_custom(_ahead)
        return ordered.find(car) + 1
    
    
    func _ahead(a: Vehicle, b: Vehicle) -> bool:
        var a_finished := finish_order.find(a)
        var b_finished := finish_order.find(b)
        if a_finished >= 0 or b_finished >= 0:
            if a_finished < 0: return false
            if b_finished < 0: return true
            return a_finished < b_finished
        if (race_status[a] == &"DNF") != (race_status[b] == &"DNF"):
            return race_status[b] == &"DNF"
        var difference := progress.total_progress(a) - progress.total_progress(b)
        return difference > 0 if not is_zero_approx(difference) else participants.find(a) < participants.find(b)
    
    
    func _end_race() -> void:
        if race_over: return
    extends Node2D
    class_name Track
    ## Iron Circuit track: geometry, static collision walls and rendering.
    ##
    ## The track is generated at runtime from a [TrackConfig] resource, so the
    ## whole layout can be reshaped in the Inspector without editing this script.
    ## Static walls are created as segment chains, exactly like the Pymunk
    ## `Segment` shapes in the Python prototype.
    
    @export var config: TrackConfig
    ## Toggle to rebuild collision walls from the config.
    @export var build_collision: bool = true
    ## Show waypoints (AI navigation targets) and the finish line marker.
    @export var show_debug: bool = false
    
    var segments: Array[Vector2] = []          # start points, parallel to segment_tangents
    var segment_lengths: PackedFloat32Array = PackedFloat32Array()
    var segment_tangents: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    var segment_normals: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    var segment_midpoints: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    var path_points: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    
    var inner_boundary: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    var outer_boundary: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
    var waypoints: Array[Dictionary] = []
    
    var total_length: float = 0.0
    var half_width: float = 0.0
    
    var finish_point: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var finish_tangent: Vector2 = Vector2.RIGHT
    var finish_normal: Vector2 = Vector2.DOWN
    var finish_line_start: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var finish_line_end: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    
    var _walls: StaticBody2D
    var _surface_line: Line2D
    var _background: Sprite2D
    var _bounds: Rect2 = Rect2()
    
    const START_GANTRY := preload("res://assets/environment/arena/start_gantry.png")
    const DECOR_TEXTURES: Array[Texture2D] = [
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/school_bus.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/semi_truck.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/tire_scrap_heap.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/crushed_cars.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/crushed_container.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/metal_fence.png"),
        preload("res://assets/environment/arena/concrete_barrier.png"),
    ]
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        if config == null:
            config = TrackConfig.new()
        half_width = config.track_width * 0.5
        _compute_geometry()
        if build_collision:
            _create_static_boundaries()
        _setup_assets()
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Geometry
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    func _compute_geometry() -> void:
        path_points = _build_smooth_path(config.centerline)
        var points := path_points
        var n := points.size()
        if n < 3:
            push_error("TrackConfig.centerline must contain at least 3 points.")
            return
    
        segments.clear()
        segment_lengths = PackedFloat32Array()
        segment_tangents = PackedVector2Array()
        segment_normals = PackedVector2Array()
        segment_midpoints = PackedVector2Array()
    
        total_length = 0.0
    
        for i in n:
            var p1 := points[i]
            var p2 := points[(i + 1) % n]
            var d := p2 - p1
            var length := d.length()
            if length <= 0.0:
                push_error("Zero-length centerline segment at index %d." % i)
                continue
    
            var tangent := d / length
            var normal := Vector2(-tangent.y, tangent.x)  # left of travel direction
    
            segments.append(p1)
            segment_lengths.append(length)
            segment_tangents.append(tangent)
            segment_normals.append(normal)
            segment_midpoints.append((p1 + p2) * 0.5)
            total_length += length
    
        # Boundaries are generated with the same round joins used by the visual
        # surface (Line2D), so the collision walls sit exactly on the visible edge.
        inner_boundary = _build_boundary(1.0)
        outer_boundary = _build_boundary(-1.0)
    
        waypoints.clear()
        for p in config.centerline:
            waypoints.append({
                "position": p,
                "radius": maxf(30.0, half_width * 0.8),
            })
    
        finish_point = points[0]
        finish_tangent = segment_tangents[0]
        finish_normal = segment_normals[0]
        finish_line_start = finish_point + finish_normal * half_width
        finish_line_end = finish_point - finish_normal * half_width
    
        _bounds = _compute_bounds()
    
    
    ## Replaces polygonal corners with quadratic arcs while preserving long
    ## straights. Dense samples are shared by rendering, physics, AI and progress.
    func _build_smooth_path(controls: PackedVector2Array) -> PackedVector2Array:
        var result := PackedVector2Array()
        if controls.size() < 3:
            return controls
        for index in controls.size():
            var previous := controls[(index - 1 + controls.size()) % controls.size()]
            var current := controls[index]
            var following := controls[(index + 1) % controls.size()]
            var incoming := current - previous
            var outgoing := following - current
            var radius := minf(160.0, minf(incoming.length() * 0.42, outgoing.length() * 0.42))
            var entry := current - incoming.normalized() * radius
            var exit := current + outgoing.normalized() * radius
            result.append(entry)
            for step in range(1, 13):
                var t := float(step) / 12.0
                var one_minus := 1.0 - t
                result.append(entry * one_minus * one_minus + current * 2.0 * one_minus * t + exit * t * t)
        return result
    
    
    ## Builds an offset boundary of the centerline on one side.
    ## `side_sign` = +1 uses the left normal, -1 the right normal. Convex corners
    ## get a sampled arc (matching a round-join ribbon), concave corners get the
    ## miter intersection of the two offset lines.
    func _build_boundary(side_sign: float) -> PackedVector2Array:
        var points := path_points
        var result := PackedVector2Array()
        if points.size() < 2:
            return points
        for index in points.size():
            var previous := points[(index - 1 + points.size()) % points.size()]
            var current := points[index]
            var following := points[(index + 1) % points.size()]
            var previous_tangent := (current - previous).normalized()
            var next_tangent := (following - current).normalized()
            var previous_normal := Vector2(-previous_tangent.y, previous_tangent.x)
            var next_normal := Vector2(-next_tangent.y, next_tangent.x)
            var normal := (previous_normal + next_normal).normalized()
            var alignment := maxf(0.5, normal.dot(next_normal))
            result.append(current + normal * half_width * side_sign / alignment)
        return result
    
    
    func _compute_bounds() -> Rect2:
        var all_points: PackedVector2Array = PackedVector2Array()
        all_points.append_array(outer_boundary)
        all_points.append_array(inner_boundary)
        if all_points.is_empty():
            return Rect2()
        var rect := Rect2(all_points[0], Vector2.ZERO)
        for p in all_points:
            rect = rect.expand(p)
        return rect
    
    
    func _create_static_boundaries() -> void:
        if _walls != null:
            _walls.queue_free()
        _walls = StaticBody2D.new()
        _walls.name = "Walls"
        var material := PhysicsMaterial.new()
        material.friction = 0.5
        material.bounce = 0.1
        _walls.physics_material_override = material
        add_child(_walls)
    
        _add_boundary_chain(outer_boundary)
        _add_boundary_chain(inner_boundary)
    
    
    func _add_boundary_chain(boundary: PackedVector2Array) -> void:
        var polygon := CollisionPolygon2D.new()
        polygon.build_mode = CollisionPolygon2D.BUILD_SEGMENTS
        polygon.polygon = boundary
        _walls.add_child(polygon)
    
    
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Assets (textures can be swapped in TrackConfig without touching logic)
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    func _setup_assets() -> void:
        _setup_background()
        _setup_surface_line()
        _setup_edge_lines()
        _setup_tire_tracks()
        _setup_trackside_decor()
        _setup_start_gantry()
    
    
    func _setup_background() -> void:
        if config.background_texture == null:
            return
        _background = Sprite2D.new()
    extends SceneTree
    ## Headless smoke test for the AI bots: they drive along the track, stay on it
    ## and open fire when a target is in front of them.
    
    var _game: Node
    var _track: Track
    var _player: Vehicle
    var _bots: Array = []
    var _start_positions: Array[Vector2] = []
    var _max_off_center: Array[float] = []
    var _fired_by: Dictionary = {}
    var _time := 0.0
    var _phase1_start := 0.0
    var _discovered := false
    var _movement_checked := false
    var _failures := 0
    
    
    func _initialize() -> void:
        _game = load("res://scenes/Main.tscn").instantiate()
        root.add_child(_game)
        _track = _game.get_node("Track")
        _player = _game.get_node("Vehicle")
    
    
    func _process(delta: float) -> bool:
        if not _discovered:
            _discovered = true
            _discover()
            if _bots.size() != 5:
                _report()
                return true
            return false
    
        _time += delta
        _track_fired()
    
        if _time < 4.0:
            _sample_off_center()
            return false
    
        if not _movement_checked:
            _movement_checked = true
            _check_movement()
            _phase1_start = _time
    
        if _time < _phase1_start + 0.8:
            # Park the player right in front of bot 0 so it actually shoots.
            var bot: BotController = _bots[0]
            var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(bot.vehicle.rotation)
            _player.global_position = bot.vehicle.global_position + forward * 150.0
            return false
    
        _check_fire_aim()
        _report()
        return true
    
    
    func _discover() -> void:
        _bots = get_nodes_in_group("bots")
        _check(_bots.size() == 5, "five bots spawned (found %d)" % _bots.size())
        for bot in _bots:
            _start_positions.append((bot as BotController).vehicle.global_position)
            _max_off_center.append(0.0)
    
    
    func _check_movement() -> void:
        for i in _bots.size():
            var bot: BotController = _bots[i]
            var moved := bot.vehicle.global_position.distance_to(_start_positions[i])
            _check(moved > 120.0, "bot %d drove along the track (%.0f px)" % [i, moved])
            _check(
                _max_off_center[i] <= _track.half_width + 8.0,
                "bot %d stayed on the track (off-centre %.1f)" % [i, _max_off_center[i]]
            )
    
    
    func _check_fire_aim() -> void:
        for i in _bots.size():
            var bot: BotController = _bots[i]
            bot.strategy = BotController.Strategy.RACER
            bot.target = _player
            var bot_vehicle := bot.vehicle
            var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(bot_vehicle.rotation)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position + forward * 150.0
            _check(bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d fires at a target ahead" % i)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position - forward * 150.0
            _check(not bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d holds fire at a target behind" % i)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position + forward * (bot.fire_range + 200.0)
            _check(not bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d holds fire out of range" % i)
    
    
    func _track_fired() -> void:
        for bullet in _game.get_node("Bullets").get_children():
            if bullet is Bullet and bullet.shooter != null and bullet.shooter != _player:
                _fired_by[bullet.shooter] = true
    
    
    func _sample_off_center() -> void:
        for i in _bots.size():
            var bot: BotController = _bots[i]
            var info := _track.get_closest_segment(bot.vehicle.global_position)
            _max_off_center[i] = maxf(_max_off_center[i], float(info["distance"]))
    
    
    func _report() -> void:
        print("BOT: %d failures" % _failures)
        print("BOT: %s" % ("PASS" if _failures == 0 else "FAIL"))
        _game.free()
        quit(0 if _failures == 0 else 1)
    
    
    func _check(condition: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if condition:
            print("BOT ok: ", label)
        else:
            _failures += 1
            print("BOT FAIL: ", label)
