    418:func get_progress_along_track(point: Vector2) -> float:
        var finish_step := config.track_width / float(finish_cells)
        for cell in finish_cells:
            var from := finish_line_end + finish_normal * finish_step * float(cell)
            var color := Color("e5d8bd") if cell % 2 == 0 else Color("292521")
            draw_line(from, from + finish_normal * finish_step, color, 8.0, true)
    
        if show_debug:
            for wp in waypoints:
                draw_arc(wp["position"], wp["radius"], 0.0, TAU, 32, config.waypoint_color, 1.0, true)
                draw_circle(wp["position"], 3.0, config.waypoint_color)
    
    
    func _draw_dashed_loop(points: PackedVector2Array, color: Color, width: float, dash: float, gap: float) -> void:
        var n := points.size()
        for i in n:
            var p1 := points[i]
            var p2 := points[(i + 1) % n]
            var seg := p2 - p1
            var seg_len := seg.length()
            if seg_len <= 0.0:
                continue
            var dir := seg / seg_len
            var dist := 0.0
            var drawing := true
            while dist < seg_len:
                var next_dist: float = minf(dist + (dash if drawing else gap), seg_len)
                if drawing:
                    draw_line(p1 + dir * dist, p1 + dir * next_dist, color, width, true)
                dist = next_dist
                drawing = not drawing
    
    
    func _build_offset_loop(offset: float) -> PackedVector2Array:
        var points := path_points
        var result := PackedVector2Array()
        for index in points.size():
            var previous := points[(index - 1 + points.size()) % points.size()]
            var current := points[index]
            var following := points[(index + 1) % points.size()]
            var previous_normal := Vector2(-(current - previous).normalized().y, (current - previous).normalized().x)
            var next_normal := Vector2(-(following - current).normalized().y, (following - current).normalized().x)
            var joined_normal := (previous_normal + next_normal).normalized()
            var alignment := maxf(0.5, joined_normal.dot(next_normal))
            result.append(current + joined_normal * offset / alignment)
        return result
    
    
    func set_debug_enabled(enabled: bool) -> void:
        show_debug = enabled
        queue_redraw()

    extends Area2D
    class_name BonusPickup
    
    signal collected(pickup: BonusPickup, vehicle: Vehicle)
    
    enum Kind { WEAPON, REPAIR, SPEED_BOOST, RAM_BOOST }
    
    @export var kind: Kind = Kind.WEAPON
    @export var weapon_duration: float = 25.0
    @export var speed_boost_duration: float = 8.0
    @export var ram_boost_duration: float = 9.0
    @export var weapon_config: WeaponConfig
    
    var armed := true
    var collected_once := false
    var _phase := 0.0
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        add_to_group("bonus_pickups")
        collision_layer = 0
        collision_mask = 1
        body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
        monitoring = armed
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func setup(new_kind: Kind, new_weapon: WeaponConfig = null, is_armed: bool = true) -> void:
        kind = new_kind
        weapon_config = new_weapon
        armed = is_armed
        if is_node_ready():
            monitoring = armed
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func set_armed(value: bool) -> void:
        armed = value
        if is_node_ready():
            monitoring = value
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func _process(delta: float) -> void:
        _phase += delta
        rotation = sin(_phase * 1.7) * 0.08
        var pulse_speed := 5.2 if armed else 2.4
        scale = Vector2.ONE * (1.0 + sin(_phase * pulse_speed) * (0.06 if armed else 0.035))
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func collect(vehicle: Vehicle) -> bool:
        if not armed or collected_once or vehicle == null or not vehicle.alive:
            return false
        collected_once = true
        match kind:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                vehicle.activate_temporary_weapon(weapon_config, weapon_duration)
            Kind.REPAIR:
                vehicle.repair()
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                vehicle.activate_boost(speed_boost_duration)
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                vehicle.activate_ram_boost(ram_boost_duration)
        collected.emit(self, vehicle)
        queue_free()
        return true
    
    
    func _on_body_entered(body: Node) -> void:
        collect(body as Vehicle)
    
    
    static func kind_label(value: Kind) -> String:
        match value:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                return "ОРУЖИЕ"
            Kind.REPAIR:
                return "РЕМОНТ"
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                return "УСКОРЕНИЕ"
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                return "УСИЛЕННЫЙ ТАРАН"
        return "БОНУС"
    
    
    func _kind_color() -> Color:
        match kind:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                return Color("ef5350")
            Kind.REPAIR:
                return Color("69d47b")
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                return Color("43c8f5")
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                return Color("ff9e2c")
        return Color.WHITE
    
    
    func _draw() -> void:
        var edge := _kind_color()
        var alpha := 0.22 if armed else 0.09
        draw_circle(Vector2.ZERO, 27.0, Color(edge, alpha))
        if not armed:
            draw_arc(Vector2.ZERO, 31.0, -PI * 0.75, PI * 0.75, 24, Color(edge, 0.55), 2.0, true)
            edge = Color(edge, 0.55)
        draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
            Vector2(0, -19), Vector2(19, 0), Vector2(0, 19), Vector2(-19, 0)
        ]), Color("182126"))
        draw_polyline(PackedVector2Array([
            Vector2(0, -19), Vector2(19, 0), Vector2(0, 19), Vector2(-19, 0), Vector2(0, -19)
        ]), edge, 3.0, true)
    
        match kind:
            Kind.WEAPON:
                draw_rect(Rect2(-11, -4, 17, 8), edge)
                draw_rect(Rect2(5, -2, 12, 4), edge)
            Kind.REPAIR:
                draw_rect(Rect2(-4, -12, 8, 24), edge)
                draw_rect(Rect2(-12, -4, 24, 8), edge)
            Kind.SPEED_BOOST:
                draw_colored_polygon(PackedVector2Array([
                    Vector2(-4, -13), Vector2(7, -13), Vector2(1, -3), Vector2(9, -3),
                    Vector2(-7, 14), Vector2(-2, 4), Vector2(-10, 4)
                ]), edge)
            Kind.RAM_BOOST:
                draw_polyline(PackedVector2Array([
                    Vector2(-12, -10), Vector2(4, -10), Vector2(12, 0), Vector2(4, 10), Vector2(-12, 10)
                ]), edge, 5.0, true)
    extends Node2D
    class_name MachineGun
    ## Fires bullets from its parent vehicle. Handles fire rate, spread and the
    ## active-bullet limit. Bullets are spawned into the node in the "bullets"
    ## group (the Bullets container in Main), so they stay in the world after the
    ## vehicle moves.
    
    signal fired
    
    @export var config: WeaponConfig
    @export var bullet_scene: PackedScene
    
    var _firing := false
    var _cooldown := 0.0
    var _bullets_parent: Node
    ## Weapons are match pickups, never part of a vehicle's starting loadout.
    var enabled := false
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        if config == null:
            config = WeaponConfig.new()
        _bullets_parent = get_tree().get_first_node_in_group("bullets")
        if _bullets_parent == null:
            _bullets_parent = get_tree().current_scene
    
    
    ## Called every physics frame by the vehicle with the current fire command.
    func set_firing(value: bool) -> void:
        _firing = value and enabled
    
    func set_enabled(value: bool) -> void:
        enabled = value
        if not enabled:
            _firing = false
    
    
    func _physics_process(delta: float) -> void:
        _cooldown = maxf(0.0, _cooldown - delta)
        if not enabled or not _firing or _cooldown > 0.0:
            return
        if _bullets_parent == null:
            _bullets_parent = get_tree().get_first_node_in_group("bullets")
            if _bullets_parent == null:
                _bullets_parent = get_tree().current_scene
        if _bullets_parent == null or bullet_scene == null:
            return
        if _active_bullet_count() >= config.max_active_bullets:
            return
        _shoot()
    
    
    func _shoot() -> void:
        var shooter := get_parent() as Vehicle
        if shooter == null:
            return
        var direction := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(shooter.global_rotation)
        if config.spread > 0.0:
            direction = direction.rotated(randf_range(-config.spread, config.spread))
        var muzzle := get_muzzle_position()
    
        var bullet: Bullet = bullet_scene.instantiate()
        _bullets_parent.add_child(bullet)
        bullet.setup(muzzle, direction, config, shooter)
    
        _cooldown = 1.0 / maxf(0.1, config.fire_rate)
        fired.emit()
    
    
    ## The projectile starts inside the front section of the body. Vehicle art can
    ## therefore contain its own integrated weapon without a separate barrel
    ## protruding beyond the silhouette.
    func get_muzzle_position() -> Vector2:
        var shooter := get_parent() as Vehicle
        if shooter == null or shooter.config == null:
            return global_position
        var body_muzzle := minf(config.muzzle_offset, shooter.config.length * 0.46)
        return shooter.to_global(Vector2(body_muzzle, 0.0))
    
    
    func _active_bullet_count() -> int:
        var count := 0
        for child in _bullets_parent.get_children():
            if child is Bullet:
                count += 1
        return count
    
    
    func get_cooldown() -> float:
        return _cooldown
    
    
    func reset() -> void:
        _firing = false
        _cooldown = 0.0

    ## Places every racer in a dedicated lane across the start line.
    func get_start_grid(count: int) -> Array[Vector2]:
        var result: Array[Vector2] = []
        if count <= 0:
            return result
        var lane_width := config.track_width / float(count)
        for index in count:
            var across := -half_width + lane_width * (float(index) + 0.5)

    ## Projects a point onto the centerline.
    ## Returns { segment_index, closest_point, t, distance }.
    func get_closest_segment(point: Vector2) -> Dictionary:
        var min_dist := INF
        var best_idx := 0
        var best_t := 0.0
        var best_point := path_points[0]
    
        for i in segment_tangents.size():
            var p1 := segments[i]
            var p2 := path_points[(i + 1) % path_points.size()]
            var d := p2 - p1
            var seg_len_sq := d.length_squared()
            var t := 0.0
            if seg_len_sq > 0.0:
                t = clampf((point - p1).dot(d) / seg_len_sq, 0.0, 1.0)
            var closest := p1 + d * t
            var dist := point.distance_to(closest)
            if dist < min_dist:
                min_dist = dist
                best_idx = i
                best_t = t
                best_point = closest
    
        return {
            "segment_index": best_idx,
            "closest_point": best_point,
            "t": best_t,
            "distance": min_dist,
        }
    
    
    ## Distance travelled along the centerline (0..total_length) for a point.
    func get_progress_along_track(point: Vector2) -> float:
        var info := get_closest_segment(point)

        var progress := 0.0
        for i in idx:
            progress += segment_lengths[i]
        progress += segment_lengths[idx] * info["t"]
        return progress

    signal impacted(intensity: float)
    signal damaged(vehicle: Vehicle, amount: float, source: Node)
    var last_attacker: Node
    
    ## Current control state (same command contract the AI will use later).
    var throttle: float = 0.0
    var brake: float = 0.0
    var steer: float = 0.0
    var fire: bool = false
    
    ## Combat state (only meaningful when the vehicle is in the "combatants" group).
    var damage := VehicleDamage.new()
    var health: float:
        get: return damage.body
        set(value): damage.body = value
    var effects: VehicleEffects
    var alive: bool = true
    var _surface_traction_factor := 1.0
    var _surface_steering_factor := 1.0
    var _surface_traction_target := 1.0
    var _surface_steering_target := 1.0
    var _surface_modifiers: Dictionary = {}
    
    var _collision_shape: CollisionShape2D
    var _velocity_debug_scale: float = 0.1
    
    @onready var gun: MachineGun = get_node_or_null("MachineGun")
    @onready var _animation_player: AnimationPlayer = get_node_or_null("AnimationPlayer")
    var _pending_reset: bool = false
    var _reset_position: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var _reset_angle: float = 0.0
    var _base_modulate: Color = Color.WHITE
    var _default_layer: int = 1
    var _default_mask: int = 1
    var _last_velocity: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var _previous_velocity: Vector2 = Vector2.ZERO
    var _collision_cooldowns: Dictionary = {}
    var weapon_time_remaining: float = 0.0
    var boost_time_remaining: float = 0.0
    var ram_boost_time_remaining: float = 0.0
    var _wheels_repair_cooldown: float = 0.0
    
    
    func _ready() -> void:
        if config == null:
            config = VehicleConfig.new()
    
        damage.reset(config)
        _base_modulate = modulate
        _default_layer = collision_layer
        _default_mask = collision_mask
    
        # Needed for car-to-car ramming damage (see _on_body_entered).
        contact_monitor = true
        max_contacts_reported = 4
        body_entered.connect(_on_body_entered)
    
        mass = config.mass
        inertia = config.moment
        gravity_scale = 0.0
        linear_damp = 0.0
        angular_damp = 0.0
        # Prevent tunnelling through thin segment walls at high speed.
        continuous_cd = RigidBody2D.CCD_MODE_CAST_SHAPE
    
        var material := PhysicsMaterial.new()
        material.friction = 0.7
        material.bounce = 0.1
        physics_material_override = material
    
        _build_collision_shape()
        effects = VehicleEffects.new()
        effects.name = "Effects"
        add_child(effects)
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func _build_collision_shape() -> void:
        if _collision_shape == null:
            _collision_shape = get_node_or_null("CollisionShape2D")
        if _collision_shape == null:
            _collision_shape = CollisionShape2D.new()
            _collision_shape.name = "CollisionShape2D"
            add_child(_collision_shape)
        var shape := ConvexPolygonShape2D.new()
        # set_point_cloud builds the convex hull, so vertex winding does not matter.
        shape.set_point_cloud(config.get_body_vertices())
        _collision_shape.shape = shape
    
    
    ## Sets the vehicle control commands. Values are clamped to their valid range.
    func apply_control(new_throttle: float, new_brake: float, new_steer: float, new_fire: bool = false) -> void:
        if not alive:
            return
        throttle = clampf(new_throttle, -1.0, 1.0)
        brake = clampf(new_brake, 0.0, 1.0)
        ram_boost_time_remaining = maxf(ram_boost_time_remaining, maxf(duration, 0.0))
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func is_ram_boost_active() -> bool:
        return alive and ram_boost_time_remaining > 0.0
    
    
    func repair(body_amount: float = 28.0, engine_amount: float = 22.0, wheels_amount: float = 24.0) -> void:
        if not alive or config == null:
            return
        damage.body = minf(config.max_health, damage.body + maxf(body_amount, 0.0))
        damage.engine = minf(config.engine_health, damage.engine + maxf(engine_amount, 0.0))
        damage.wheels = minf(config.wheels_health, damage.wheels + maxf(wheels_amount, 0.0))
        _wheels_repair_cooldown = 0.0
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func repair_full() -> void:
        if not alive or config == null:
            return
        damage.reset(config)
        _wheels_repair_cooldown = 0.0
        if effects != null:
            effects.reset()
        queue_redraw()
    
    
    func has_temporary_weapon() -> bool:
        return alive and weapon_time_remaining > 0.0 and gun != null and gun.enabled
    
    
    func get_current_max_speed() -> float:
        if config == null:
            return 1.0
        return minf(config.max_speed * (config.boost_speed_multiplier if is_boost_active() else 1.0), 200.0)
    
    
    func _update_temporary_bonuses(delta: float) -> void:
        if weapon_time_remaining > 0.0:
            weapon_time_remaining = maxf(0.0, weapon_time_remaining - delta)
            if weapon_time_remaining <= 0.0 and gun != null:
                gun.set_enabled(false)
                gun.set_firing(false)
        if boost_time_remaining > 0.0:
            boost_time_remaining = maxf(0.0, boost_time_remaining - delta)
        if ram_boost_time_remaining > 0.0:
            ram_boost_time_remaining = maxf(0.0, ram_boost_time_remaining - delta)
        queue_redraw()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    # Restart helper

    ## Places the vehicle at a start position and clears all motion.
    ##
    ## The actual teleport is applied on the next physics step (see
    ## `_integrate_forces`), which is the only safe way to move a RigidBody2D.
    func reset_to(position: Vector2, angle: float) -> void:
        alive = true
        last_attacker = null
        damage.reset(config)
        if effects != null:
            effects.reset()
        modulate = _base_modulate
        collision_layer = _default_layer
        collision_mask = _default_mask
        freeze = false
        if _animation_player != null:
            _animation_player.stop()
        if gun != null:
            gun.reset()
            gun.set_enabled(false)
            gun.set_physics_process(true)
        weapon_time_remaining = 0.0
        boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        ram_boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        _wheels_repair_cooldown = 0.0
        throttle = 0.0
        brake = 0.0
        steer = 0.0
        fire = false
        sleeping = false
        _reset_position = position

        angular_velocity = 0.0
        _last_velocity = Vector2.ZERO
        _previous_velocity = Vector2.ZERO

    ## Sets the vehicle control commands. Values are clamped to their valid range.
    func apply_control(new_throttle: float, new_brake: float, new_steer: float, new_fire: bool = false) -> void:
        if not alive:
            return
        throttle = clampf(new_throttle, -1.0, 1.0)
        brake = clampf(new_brake, 0.0, 1.0)
        steer = clampf(new_steer, -1.0, 1.0)
        fire = new_fire
        if gun != null:
            gun.set_firing(fire)
    
    func set_surface_modifier(traction_multiplier: float, steering_multiplier: float, source: Node = null) -> void:
        var key: Variant = source if source != null else &"default"
        _surface_modifiers[key] = Vector2(
            clampf(traction_multiplier, 0.1, 1.0),
            clampf(steering_multiplier, 0.1, 1.0)
        )
        _recalculate_surface_modifier()
    
    func clear_surface_modifier(source: Node = null) -> void:
        var key: Variant = source if source != null else &"default"
        _surface_modifiers.erase(key)
        _recalculate_surface_modifier()
    
    func _recalculate_surface_modifier() -> void:
        _surface_traction_target = 1.0
        _surface_steering_target = 1.0
        for modifier in _surface_modifiers.values():
            _surface_traction_target = minf(_surface_traction_target, modifier.x)
            _surface_steering_target = minf(_surface_steering_target, modifier.y)
    
    func get_surface_traction_factor() -> float:
        return _surface_traction_factor
    
    func get_surface_steering_factor() -> float:
        return _surface_steering_factor
    
    
    ## Shared damage entry point for both modes. Ignored once destroyed.
    func take_damage(amount: float, module: StringName = &"body", source: Node = null) -> void:
        if not alive or amount <= 0.0 or not is_finite(amount):
            return
        amount *= 1.0 - clampf(config.damage_resistance, 0.0, 0.75)
        var before := health
        var wheels_before := damage.wheels
        damage.apply(amount, module)
        if damage.wheels < wheels_before:
            _wheels_repair_cooldown = config.wheels_repair_delay
        if health < before:
            last_attacker = source
            damaged.emit(self, before - health, source)
        if effects != null:
            effects.impact(clampf(amount / config.max_health, 0.1, 1.0))
        if health <= 0.0:
            _die()
        elif _animation_player != null and _animation_player.has_animation("hit_flash"):
            _animation_player.play("hit_flash")
    
    
    func _die() -> void:
        if not alive: return
        alive = false
        remove_from_group("combatants")
        throttle = 0.0
        brake = 0.0
        steer = 0.0
        fire = false
        if gun != null:
            gun.set_firing(false)
            gun.set_enabled(false)
            gun.set_physics_process(false)
        weapon_time_remaining = 0.0
        boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        ram_boost_time_remaining = 0.0
        linear_velocity = Vector2.ZERO
        angular_velocity = 0.0
        # Changing physics state mid-step must be deferred.
        call_deferred("_disable_destroyed_physics")
        if effects != null:
            effects.explode()
        if _animation_player != null and _animation_player.has_animation("destroyed"):
            _animation_player.play("destroyed")
        died.emit(self)

    ## All tunable parameters for a vehicle.
    ##
    ## This resource is the Godot replacement for the Python `config.py` vehicle
    ## block. Edit the values in the Inspector (or create additional `.tres`
    ## resources) without touching any code.
    
    @export_group("Dimensions")
    @export_range(4.0, 400.0, 0.5) var length: float = 40.0
    @export_range(4.0, 400.0, 0.5) var width: float = 24.0
    ## Bevel applied to the four corners of the collision octagon (0 = sharp box).
    @export_range(0.0, 50.0, 0.5) var corner_bevel_ratio: float = 0.15
    
    @export_group("Body")
    @export_range(1.0, 100000.0, 1.0) var mass: float = 1000.0
    ## 0 lets Godot compute the moment of inertia from the collision shape.
    @export_range(0.0, 100000000.0, 1.0) var moment: float = 180000.0
    
    @export_group("Movement")
    @export_range(1.0, 200.0, 1.0) var max_speed: float = 160.0
    @export_range(1.0, 20000.0, 1.0) var acceleration: float = 800.0
    @export_range(1.0, 20000.0, 1.0) var brake_force: float = 1200.0
    ## Fraction of forward speed kept per physics step (rolling resistance).
    @export_range(0.5, 1.0, 0.001) var drag: float = 0.98
    ## Lateral (sideways) grip coefficient. Higher = less sliding.
    @export_range(0.0, 200.0, 0.1) var traction: float = 15.0
    
    @export_group("Steering")
    @export_range(0.0, 50.0, 0.1) var steer_rate: float = 5.0
    ## Speed (px/s) at which steering is most effective.
    @export_range(0.01, 1000.0, 0.01) var steer_speed_factor: float = 0.3
    ## Fraction of angular velocity kept per physics step.
    @export_range(0.5, 1.0, 0.001) var angular_damping: float = 0.95
    
    @export_group("Combat")
    ## Hit points used in survival / battle-royale modes. Unused in race mode.
    @export_range(1.0, 1000.0, 1.0) var max_health: float = 100.0
    ## Fraction of incoming damage absorbed by armour (0.2 = 20%).
    @export_range(0.0, 0.75, 0.01) var damage_resistance: float = 0.0
    
    @export_group("Ramming")
    ## Legacy linear-impact tuning retained for resource compatibility.
    @export_range(0.0, 5.0, 0.01) var collision_damage: float = 0.18
    @export_range(0.0, 1000.0, 5.0) var collision_min_speed: float = 70.0
    ## Closing speed below which contact is only cosmetic.
    @export_range(0.0, 200.0, 1.0) var impact_safe_speed: float = 25.0
    ## Closing speed that reaches maximum base collision damage.
    @export_range(25.0, 500.0, 5.0) var impact_damage_full_speed: float = 125.0
    @export_range(0.0, 20.0, 0.5) var impact_min_damage: float = 5.0
    @export_range(1.0, 100.0, 1.0) var impact_max_damage: float = 68.0
    @export_range(1.0, 100.0, 1.0) var impact_damage_cap: float = 78.0
    ## Multiplies damage delivered by this vehicle to another vehicle.
    @export_range(0.25, 3.0, 0.05) var ram_power: float = 1.0
    @export_range(0.25, 2.0, 0.05) var front_impact_factor: float = 0.7
    @export_range(0.25, 2.0, 0.05) var side_impact_factor: float = 1.35
    @export_range(0.25, 2.0, 0.05) var rear_impact_factor: float = 1.15
    
    @export_group("Looks")
    @export var body_color: Color = Color(0.0, 0.784, 0.392)
    @export var outline_color: Color = Color(0.0, 0.35, 0.18)
    @export var front_marker_color: Color = Color(1.0, 1.0, 0.0)
    @export var velocity_vector_color: Color = Color(1.0, 0.392, 0.392)
    
    
    ## Builds the local-space collision/render vertices of the bevelled rectangle.
    func get_body_vertices() -> PackedVector2Array:
        var half_l := length * 0.5
        var half_w := width * 0.5
        var bevel: float = minf(length, width) * corner_bevel_ratio
        return PackedVector2Array([
            Vector2(half_l - bevel, half_w),
            Vector2(half_l, half_w - bevel),
            Vector2(half_l, -half_w + bevel),
            Vector2(half_l - bevel, -half_w),
            Vector2(-half_l + bevel, -half_w),
            Vector2(-half_l, -half_w + bevel),
            Vector2(-half_l, half_w - bevel),
            Vector2(-half_l + bevel, half_w),
        ])
    
    
    @export_group("Assets")
    ## Optional sprite. When set it is drawn instead of the procedural shape, so
    ## the car image can be replaced without touching any game logic.
    @export var texture: Texture2D
    
    @export_group("Damage modules")
    @export_range(1.0, 1000.0) var engine_health: float = 70.0
    @export_range(1.0, 1000.0) var wheels_health: float = 60.0
    @export_range(0.0, 1.0) var engine_damage_share: float = 0.55
    @export_range(0.0, 1.0) var wheels_damage_share: float = 0.45
    @export_range(0.05, 1.0) var engine_min_factor: float = 0.3
    @export_range(0.05, 1.0) var steering_min_factor: float = 0.45
    @export_range(0.05, 1.0) var traction_min_factor: float = 0.25
    ## Wheels recover automatically after damage. Upgrades increase this value.
    @export_range(0.0, 30.0, 0.1) var wheels_repair_rate: float = 1.5
    @export_range(0.0, 15.0, 0.1) var wheels_repair_delay: float = 4.0
    
    @export_group("Temporary boost")
    @export_range(1.0, 2.0, 0.05) var boost_speed_multiplier: float = 1.25
    @export_range(1.0, 3.0, 0.05) var boost_acceleration_multiplier: float = 1.65
    @export_range(1.0, 3.0, 0.05) var boost_ram_multiplier: float = 1.35
    
    @export_group("Feedback")
    @export var effects_enabled: bool = true
    @export var debug_vectors: bool = false

    extends SceneTree
    ## Headless smoke test for Track geometry and queries.
    
    var _track: Track
    var _frames := 0
    
    
    func _initialize() -> void:
        _track = Track.new()
        _track.config = load("res://resources/iron_circuit.tres")
        root.add_child(_track)
    
    
    func _process(_delta: float) -> bool:
        _frames += 1
        if _frames < 3:
            return false
    
        var failures := 0
        failures += _check(_track.outer_boundary.size() >= _track.config.centerline.size(), "outer boundary count")
        failures += _check(_track.inner_boundary.size() >= _track.config.centerline.size(), "inner boundary count")
        failures += _check(_track.total_length > 0.0, "total length")
        failures += _check(_track.get_total_length() == _track.total_length, "total length accessor")
        failures += _check(_track.waypoints.size() == _track.config.centerline.size(), "waypoints")
        failures += _check(_track.finish_tangent.length() > 0.99, "finish tangent")
        failures += _check(_track.is_point_on_track(_track.get_start_position()), "start on track")
    
        var info := _track.get_closest_segment(_track.get_start_position())
        failures += _check(info["distance"] < 0.001, "start projects onto centerline")
        var progress := _track.get_progress_along_track(_track.get_start_position())
        failures += _check(progress < 1.0, "start progress ~0")
    
        var max_err := 0.0
        for boundary in [_track.inner_boundary, _track.outer_boundary]:
            for p in boundary:
                var d: float = _track.get_closest_segment(p)["distance"]
                max_err = maxf(max_err, absf(d - _track.half_width))
        failures += _check(max_err < 8.0, "smoothed boundaries follow half width (err=%.3f)" % max_err)
        failures += _check(_track.inner_boundary.size() >= _track.config.centerline.size(), "inner boundary has corner arcs")
        failures += _check(_track.outer_boundary.size() >= _track.config.centerline.size(), "outer boundary has corner arcs")
    
        print("TRACK: %d failures" % failures)
        print("TRACK: %s" % ("PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL"))
        _track.free()
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
        return true
    
    
    func _check(condition: bool, label: String) -> int:
        if not condition:
            print("TRACK FAIL: ", label)
            return 1
        return 0
    extends SceneTree
    
    var failures := 0
    
    func _initialize() -> void:
        call_deferred("run")
    
    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("VEHICLE PROGRESSION FAIL: ", label)
    
    func run() -> void:
        for id in LoadoutProfile.VEHICLE_IDS:
            check(LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(id).max_speed <= 200.0, "%s base speed capped" % id)
        var upgraded := PlayerProfile.apply_vehicle_upgrades_from(LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(&"viper"), [&"engine_1"])
        check(upgraded.max_speed <= 200.0, "engine upgrade respects 200 km/h cap")
        check(upgraded.acceleration > LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(&"viper").acceleration, "engine improves dynamics")
        var wheel_upgrade := PlayerProfile.apply_vehicle_upgrades_from(LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(&"raider"), [&"wheels_1"])
        check(wheel_upgrade.wheels_repair_rate > LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(&"raider").wheels_repair_rate, "wheel upgrade improves repair")
        var armor_upgrade := PlayerProfile.apply_vehicle_upgrades_from(LoadoutProfile.vehicle_config(&"raider"), [&"armor_1"])
        check(armor_upgrade.damage_resistance > 0.0, "armor absorbs damage")
        var vehicle := (load("res://scenes/Vehicle.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Vehicle
        vehicle.config = wheel_upgrade
        root.add_child(vehicle)
        await process_frame
        vehicle.take_damage(20.0, &"wheels")
        var damaged_wheels := vehicle.damage.wheels
        vehicle._wheels_repair_cooldown = 0.0
        vehicle._physics_process(1.0)
        check(vehicle.damage.wheels > damaged_wheels, "damaged wheels repair over time")
        var strip := (load("res://scenes/SpikeStrip.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as SpikeStrip
        root.add_child(strip)
        var before_spikes := vehicle.damage.wheels
        strip._on_body_entered(vehicle)
        check(vehicle.damage.wheels < before_spikes, "spike strip damages wheels")
        vehicle.free()
    
        var race := (load("res://scenes/Main.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Game
        root.add_child(race)
        await process_frame
        check(race.participants.size() == 6, "race has player and five bots")
        var strategies := {}
        for bot in get_nodes_in_group("bots"):
            strategies[(bot as BotController).strategy] = true
        check(strategies.size() == 5, "all five race bots use unique strategies")
        race.free()
    
        var survival := (load("res://scenes/ArenaBattle.tscn") as PackedScene).instantiate() as Survival
        root.add_child(survival)
        await process_frame
        check(survival._enemies.size() == 5, "survival has five bots")
        var arena_strategies := {}
        for bot in get_nodes_in_group("arena_bots"):
            arena_strategies[(bot as ArenaBot).strategy] = true
        check(arena_strategies.size() >= 4, "arena bots use distinct strategies")
        print("VEHICLE PROGRESSION: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)

    extends SceneTree
    var failures := 0
    func _initialize() -> void: call_deferred("run")
    func check(ok: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if not ok:
            failures += 1
            printerr("RACE FINISH FAIL: ", label)
    func run() -> void:
        var original_loadout := LoadoutProfile.read_ids()
        LoadoutProfile.save_ids(&"raider", &"machine_gun")
        change_scene_to_file("res://scenes/Main.tscn")
        await scene_changed
        var game: Game = current_scene
        await create_timer(0.1).timeout
        game.set_physics_process(false)
        for bot in game._bots: bot.set_physics_process(false)
        for car in game.participants: car.freeze = true
        var p := game.vehicle
        var bot: Vehicle = game.participants[1]
        var initial_position := game.get_race_position(p)
        check(initial_position == 6, "initial progress ranking")
        p.position = point_at(game.track, 20.0)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        p.position = point_at(game.track, 90.0)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        p.position = point_at(game.track, 175.0)
        game.progress.advance(p, 1.0 / 60)
        check(game.get_race_position(p) <= 3, "ranking updates after overtaking three racers")
        # Complete actual sampled laps through the same physics coordinator API.
        game._reset_race()
        game.set_physics_process(false)
        for driver in game._bots: driver.set_physics_process(false)
        for car in game.participants:
            car.freeze = true
            car._pending_reset = false
        var length := game.track.get_total_length()
        p.take_damage(45.0)
        p.take_damage(20.0, &"engine")
        for lap in range(3):
            for i in range(1, 201):
                p.position = point_at(game.track, length * i / 200.0 + 0.1)
                game._physics_process(1.0 / 60)
            check(p.damage.ratio(&"body") == 1.0 and p.damage.ratio(&"engine") == 1.0 and p.damage.ratio(&"wheels") == 1.0, "lap crossing fully repairs all modules")
        check(game.race_over and game.race_status[p] == &"FINISHED", "finish lifecycle")
        check(game.results.visible and game.result.laps == 3, "results shown with 3 laps")
        check(game.result.time > 0 and game.result.position == 1, "result time and rank")
        var final_time := game.elapsed
        game._physics_process(1)
        check(game.elapsed == final_time, "timer stops")
        game.results.get_node("%RetryButton").pressed.emit()
        await process_frame
        check(not game.race_over and not game.results.visible and p.alive and not p.freeze, "Retry fully restores")
        check(game.elapsed == 0 and game.progress.laps(p) == 0, "Retry clears race")
        # Real lethal projectile exercises result creation during Bullet._check_hit.
        for car in game.participants: car.freeze = true
        for index in range(2, game.participants.size()):
            game.participants[index].position = Vector2(600, 400 + index * 20)
        p.position = Vector2(440, 150)
        bot.position = Vector2(300, 150)
        bot.rotation = 0
        bot.gun.config = bot.gun.config.duplicate()
        bot.gun.config.bullet_damage = 100
        bot.gun._shoot()
        await create_timer(0.4).timeout
        check(game.race_over and game.result.status == &"DNF" and game.results.visible, "projectile DNF results")
        game.results.get_node("%GarageButton").pressed.emit()
        await scene_changed
        check(current_scene.scene_file_path == "res://scenes/Garage.tscn", "result Garage action")
        change_scene_to_file("res://scenes/Main.tscn")
        await scene_changed
        game = current_scene
        game.vehicle.take_damage(1000)
        check(game.result.status == &"DNF", "immediate death result")
        game.results.get_node("%MenuButton").pressed.emit()
        await scene_changed
        check(current_scene.scene_file_path == "res://scenes/MainMenu.tscn", "result Menu action")
        current_scene.free()
        LoadoutProfile.save_ids(original_loadout.vehicle, original_loadout.weapon)
        print("RACE FINISH: ", "PASS" if failures == 0 else "FAIL")
        quit(0 if failures == 0 else 1)
    
    func point_at(track: Track, distance: float) -> Vector2:
        var remaining := fposmod(distance, track.get_total_length())
        for i in track.segment_lengths.size():
            if remaining <= track.segment_lengths[i]:
                return track.segments[i] + track.segment_tangents[i] * remaining
            remaining -= track.segment_lengths[i]
        return track.finish_point
            )
    
    
    func _check_fire_aim() -> void:
        for i in _bots.size():
            var bot: BotController = _bots[i]
            bot.strategy = BotController.Strategy.RACER
            bot.target = _player
            var bot_vehicle := bot.vehicle
            var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(bot_vehicle.rotation)
            bot_vehicle.activate_temporary_weapon(bot_vehicle.gun.config, 5.0)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position + forward * 150.0
            _check(bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d fires at a target ahead" % i)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position - forward * 150.0
            _check(not bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d holds fire at a target behind" % i)
    
            _player.global_position = bot_vehicle.global_position + forward * (bot.fire_range + 200.0)
            _check(not bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d holds fire out of range" % i)
            bot_vehicle.gun.set_enabled(false)
            _check(not bot.get_commands(0.0)["fire"], "bot %d only fires with a weapon bonus" % i)
    
    
    func _track_fired() -> void:
        for bullet in _game.get_node("Bullets").get_children():
            if bullet is Bullet and bullet.shooter != null and bullet.shooter != _player:
                _fired_by[bullet.shooter] = true
    
    
    func _sample_off_center() -> void:
        for i in _bots.size():
            var bot: BotController = _bots[i]
            var info := _track.get_closest_segment(bot.vehicle.global_position)
            _max_off_center[i] = maxf(_max_off_center[i], float(info["distance"]))
    
    
    func _report() -> void:
        print("BOT: %d failures" % _failures)
        print("BOT: %s" % ("PASS" if _failures == 0 else "FAIL"))
        _game.free()
        quit(0 if _failures == 0 else 1)
    
    
    func _check(condition: bool, label: String) -> void:
        if condition:

Проверка обнаружила два важных момента. Без продольного смещения старт стал безопасным, но сломалась исходная честная очередность позиций; верну смещение, рассчитывая каждую точку по реальной центральной линии и её локальной нормали — так фиолетовая машина останется внутри трассы. Также уточняю прицел: назначенный соперник прямо впереди должен считаться валидной целью независимо от неоднозначности проекции на близких участках извилистой трассы.

    $ sed -n '155,235p' scripts/bot_controller.gd && sed -n '360,390p' scripts/track.gd
        var idx: int = info["segment_index"]
        var count := track.segment_lengths.size()
        if count == 0:
            return from
    
        var t: float = float(info["t"])
        var remaining := distance
        var current_remaining := track.segment_lengths[idx] * (1.0 - t)
        if remaining <= current_remaining:
            var p1: Vector2 = track.segments[idx]
            var p2: Vector2 = track.path_points[(idx + 1) % count]
            var local_t := t + remaining / track.segment_lengths[idx]
            return p1.lerp(p2, clampf(local_t, 0.0, 1.0))
    
        remaining -= current_remaining
        var i := (idx + 1) % count
        var guard := 0
        while remaining > track.segment_lengths[i] and guard < count:
            remaining -= track.segment_lengths[i]
            i = (i + 1) % count
            guard += 1
        var p1_next: Vector2 = track.segments[i]
        var p2_next: Vector2 = track.path_points[(i + 1) % count]
        return p1_next.lerp(p2_next, clampf(remaining / track.segment_lengths[i], 0.0, 1.0))
    
    
    func _should_fire() -> bool:
        if strategy == Strategy.SPRINTER:
            return false
        if vehicle == null or not vehicle.has_temporary_weapon():
            return false
        # The injected rival is reliable when it is physically in the firing cone;
        # this avoids ambiguous progress projections where two bends run nearby.
        if _is_in_fire_solution(target):
            return true
        var victim := _combat_target()
        return _is_in_fire_solution(victim)
    
    
    func _is_in_fire_solution(victim: Vehicle) -> bool:
        if victim == null or not is_instance_valid(victim) or not victim.alive:
            return false
        var to_target := victim.global_position - vehicle.global_position
        var distance := to_target.length()
        if distance > fire_range or distance < 0.001:
            return false
        var forward := Vector2.RIGHT.rotated(vehicle.rotation)
        return absf(forward.angle_to(to_target)) <= fire_angle
    
    func _apply_strategy() -> void:
        match strategy:
            Strategy.RACER:
                cruise_throttle = 0.96
                lookahead = 135.0
                ease_angle = 0.58
                _lane_bias = -0.48
            Strategy.BRAWLER:
                cruise_throttle = 1.0
                lookahead = 80.0
                fire_angle = 0.34
                fire_range = 360.0
                _lane_bias = -0.18
            Strategy.OPPORTUNIST:
                lookahead = 95.0
                fire_range = 560.0
                fire_angle = 0.26
                _lane_bias = 0.18
            Strategy.BLOCKER:
                cruise_throttle = 0.92
                lookahead = 105.0
                fire_range = 420.0
                fire_angle = 0.3
                _lane_bias = 0.48
            Strategy.SPRINTER:
                cruise_throttle = 1.0
                lookahead = 165.0
                ease_angle = 0.72
                brake_angle = 1.48
                steer_gain = 2.2
                _lane_bias = 0.0
    
    func get_start_angle() -> float:
        return finish_tangent.angle()
    
    
    ## Places every racer in a dedicated lane across the start line.  The usable
    ## width deliberately excludes the vehicle half-width plus a visual margin;
    ## previously the last slot was also staggered far into the first bend, which
    ## could place the purple car beyond the outer wall.
    func get_start_grid(count: int) -> Array[Vector2]:
        var result: Array[Vector2] = []
        if count <= 0:
            return result
        var edge_margin := minf(28.0, half_width * 0.3)
        var usable_half_width := maxf(0.0, half_width - edge_margin)
        var grid_step := minf(28.0, config.track_width / float(count) * 0.8)
        for index in count:
            var ratio := 0.5 if count == 1 else float(index) / float(count - 1)
            var across := lerpf(-usable_half_width, usable_half_width, ratio)
            var centre := get_point_at_distance(grid_step * float(index))
            var centre_info := get_closest_segment(centre)
            var local_normal := segment_normals[int(centre_info.segment_index)]
            result.append(centre + local_normal * across)
        return result
    
    
    func get_bounds() -> Rect2:
        return _bounds
    
    
    func get_total_length() -> float:
        return total_length

Готово.

- Исправил стартовую сетку: каждая позиция рассчитывается по реальной траектории и локальному направлению трассы.
- Добавлен безопасный отступ от границ. Фиолетовая машина теперь стартует внутри трассы.
- Главный приоритет ботов — прохождение кругов и удержание гоночной линии.
- При отклонении бот сначала возвращается в безопасный коридор.
- Бонусы подбираются только тогда, когда находятся по ходу движения.
- Таран применяется против соперника впереди и только без выезда с трассы.
- Стрельба ведётся только при активном оружейном бонусе.
- Боты не стреляют в машины позади или за пределами дальности.

Тесты трассы, прохождения гонки, боевой системы и поведения ботов прошли. Визуально проверил фиолетовую машину на старте и после начала движения — она остаётся на трассе. Обновлённая игра запущена.
